# Intuitionistic PRA proof-existence target

The active objective is a Lean proof that the chosen literal equation's
no-positive-solution sentence is equivalent to the chosen formal `Con(ZFC)`
sentence in **intuitionistic** primitive recursive arithmetic. This is a
proof-existence statement, not a request to print a proof and not a proof of
either side separately. Literal iPRA endpoint theorems have passed matching
fixed-snapshot replays. The identified project-object mismatch has since been
repaired on that snapshot, and the 14-variable endpoint rechecked on the
repaired parent; source-current and inherited external-ancestry qualifications
remain as described below.

A [nine-natural-coordinate Jones expression built from the 33-witness seed](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ipra-endpoint-check/README.md)
now has [independently audited Lean endpoints](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ipra-endpoint-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md)
for the native and standard iPRA mapped-refutation equivalences and the
standard mapped Gödel–Gentzen consistency equivalence. The symbolic expression
is `ExecutableJones.expression (SharingSeed33.seed CurrentWindowCarry2011.ports) 0`;
its nine coordinates range over naturals including zero. The Lean source's
historical uncompiled-provider header is stale, as documented in its README.
The checked endpoint does not bind a literal numerical equation or PDF, nor
establish an operation count or exact degree. The retained initial-agreement
ancestor has successful Lean child exits but no captured numeric outer exit;
inherited provider and point-observation limits remain.
A [33-seed named-formula client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-jones-named-formula-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-jones-named-formula-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the symbolic named Lean right-hand side over every commutative ring.
It does not bind an external serialization or establish a degree theorem.
An [external degree survey](research/nine-seed33-jones-metric-survey/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-metric-transcription-frontier-independent/README.md),
gives the conservative upper bound `291404·5^34 + 860 =
169619452208280563354492188360` for the symbolic nine-variable expression.
It is not a Lean theorem, exact degree, operation count or literal-export
binding; its seed fingerprints are source/AST fingerprints.
A [33-seed compact macro prototype](research/nine-seed33-jones-compact-macro-prototype/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-compact-macro-frontier-independent/README.md),
records 45 live named nodes with the seed-specific symbolic coefficients.
This is a named-node count, not an arithmetic operation count; the JSON is not
formally bound to the Lean formula. A [macro cost survey](research/nine-seed33-jones-macro-cost-survey/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-macro-cost-frontier-independent/README.md),
counts 173 explicit operators when powers and finite sums each cost one unit
and named references are shared. This convention is not a binary circuit or
expanded-sum operation count, and the JSON remains unbound to Lean.
A [specialized sign33 macro source draft](research/nine-seed33-jones-compact-lean-specialized-draft/README.md),
[independently reviewed](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-compact-lean-specialized-frontier-independent/README.md),
quotes the 45-node prototype and specializes the checked sign38 macro
language. Its Lean elaboration and semantic targets remain unproved; the
source review alone is not a formal JSON or human-text binding.
A [language compilation attempt](research/nine-seed33-jones-compact-language-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-compact-language-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
hit Lean's interpreter memory limit under `-M2800` and produced no object or
final postflight; the responsible declaration or dependency is unknown. A
[reduced-import retry](research/nine-seed33-jones-compact-language-import-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-compact-language-import-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
compiles the specialized macro-language definitions under the same limits.
A [recipe/scopedness client](research/nine-seed33-jones-compact-recipe-scoped-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-compact-recipe-scoped-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
compiles the 45-node Lean recipe and checks its Boolean scopedness without
axioms. A [generic cuts client](research/nine-seed33-jones-compact-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-compact-cuts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the snoc, range, star and prefix-lookup identities. Concrete recipe
semantic equality and external serialization binding remain separate. A
[named-prefix client](research/nine-seed33-jones-named-prefix-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-named-prefix-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that the Lean recipe equals its 45-node named-prefix representation;
its theorem is axiom-free. A [first-value client](research/nine-seed33-jones-first-value-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-first-value-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the first short prefix's value. Neither establishes full evaluator or
JSON equality. A [first-entry preservation client](research/nine-seed33-jones-first-preservation-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-first-preservation-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that this first value persists through the full Lean recipe; full output
semantics remain separate. A [B/q/N prefix client](research/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group01-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the next three short-prefix values. A [second prefix group](research/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group02-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group02-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks three B carries and the e/ell/lambda values; full output semantics
remain separate. A [third prefix group](research/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group03-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group03-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks further carry, g, theta and G identities, still locally. A
[fourth prefix group](research/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group04-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group04-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks seven input-prefix values; full recipe output semantics remain open.
A [q/N preservation client](research/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group05-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group05-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks their values at the later d10 prefix. A [d10 carry group](research/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group06-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-prefix-group06-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),

A [human-readable 33-seed formula](research/nine-seed33-jones-human-formula/FORMULA.md)
has passed an [independent manual transcription review](reviews/nine-seed33-jones-human-formula-frontier-independent/README.md).
Lean does not parse the Markdown; numeric expansion and exact metrics remain
separate.
A [bounded signed-cut search](research/nine-sharing-sign33-cut-search/README.md),
[independently reviewed](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-cut-search-frontier-independent/README.md),
found no candidate below 33 witnesses in its sampled space. Its seeded search
is not reproducible as originally stated because hash-dependent set ordering
affects the trajectory. This is neither an exhaustive search nor a lower bound;
selected signed cuts still require residual construction and sign proofs.
A [sorted-baseline retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-cut-search-deterministic/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-cut-search-deterministic-frontier-independent/README.md),
reproduces byte-for-byte in isolated runs and again finds best 33 in the
restricted sample. It remains a finite heuristic, not a lower bound or proof
of the candidate's signed-cut hypotheses.
A separate [32-witness sextic candidate](research/nine-sharing-sign32-sextic-candidate-v2/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-sextic-frontier-independent/README.md),
eliminates the `q²` cut by substitution. The external audit checks 23
residuals, the coefficient bytes and the exact degree-six argument over the
fixed integer ports; the earlier candidate's unresolved relative coefficient
path is retained in its own packet. Both all-model reconstruction directions
remain to be checked in Lean. The existing nine-variable Jones theorem requires
degree at most four, so no 32-witness Jones endpoint follows from this result.
A [source-only Lean syntax and bridge draft](research/nine-sharing-sign32-sextic-lean-draft/README.md),
[independently reviewed](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-sextic-lean-frontier-independent/README.md),
transcribes all 23 residuals and defines proposed lift/drop maps and two open
zero-set propositions. Separate errata correct its reproduction instructions;
neither bridge direction has been proved in Lean. A [syntax and square-sum
client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-seed-squares-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-seed-squares-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that symbolic 32-seed zero is equivalent to zero of all 23 residuals at
the same valuation. A [first substitution client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group00-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group00-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that the removed q² residual vanishes under the lift and matches the
first retained q³ residual. The other substitution identities, 32-to-33 bridge
and endpoint remain separate. A [second substitution client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group01-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the X/Y/E residual identities and list positions; it records six linter
warnings. A [third substitution client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group02-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the kY/ell/Delta residual identities and list positions; it records
eight linter warnings. A [fourth substitution client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group03-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group03-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks mainRoot/c²/Delta-c² identities and list positions; it records eight
linter warnings. A [fifth substitution client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group04-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group04-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks N1/gap/tau identities and list positions, retaining the beta-port cast
for tau; it records three linter warnings. The remaining bridge obligations
stay separate. A [sixth substitution client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group05-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group05-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks N0/Nt/f² identities and list positions, recording four linter warnings.
A [seventh substitution client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group06-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group06-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks cTminusR/V/normalizedS identities and list positions, retaining the
beta-port cast for cTminusR and recording four warnings. The remaining bridge
obligations stay separate. A [final substitution client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group07-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-substitution-group07-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the last four residual identities and list positions, recording 12
linter warnings. All 23 individual comparisons are checked; assembly and
backward coordinate reconstruction remain separate. A [forward assembly](research/nine-sharing-sign32-forward-assembly-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-forward-assembly-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that 32-seed zero implies 33-seed zero at the lift in an IΣ₁ model
under the beta-port cast. The reverse direction and fixed-port specialization
remain separate. A [coordinate-recovery client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-coordinate-recovery-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-coordinate-recovery-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that old-seed zero and the beta cast force the removed q² coordinate
and `lift (drop v) = v`. A [reverse assembly](research/nine-sharing-sign32-reverse-assembly-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-reverse-assembly-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks old 33-seed zero implies 32-seed zero at the drop under that cast.
A [seed equivalence client](research/nine-sharing-sign32-seed-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-seed-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
packages the pointwise lift equivalence and same-input existential equivalence
in IΣ₁ models under the explicit beta cast. Fixed-port specialization and a
sextic-compatible Jones endpoint remain separate. A [current-port specialization](research/nine-sharing-sign32-current-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-current-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
discharges the beta cast and checks both equivalences for current ports. Its
current-data route inherits successful rows from an overall-failed split and
the recorded ancestry limits; external JSON binding and a sextic-compatible
Jones endpoint remain separate. A [32-seed/source composition](research/nine-sharing-sign32-current-source-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-current-source-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that a current sextic seed zero with 32 natural tail witnesses and
input 1 is equivalent to current source zero in IΣ₁ models; it records one
unused-section-variable warning. The direct 32-coordinate endpoint and any
sextic-compatible Jones endpoint remain separate. A [direct endpoint attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed in its first stage with a Lean interpreter memory exception under
`-M2800`; it produced no expression object or theorem, and the endpoint stage
and final postflight did not run. The specific source trigger is unknown. A
[factored retry](research/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-retry01-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
compiled the generic input-substitution evaluation, but its concrete
expression/evaluation stage again hit the interpreter memory limit. Its
endpoint and final postflight did not run; the precise trigger remains unknown.
A further [abstract-port retry](research/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-abstract-retry/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-abstract-retry-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
also hit the interpreter memory limit in its first, symbolic-port stage, which
contains both a definition and its evaluation theorem. No object or endpoint
result was produced; the triggering declaration remains unknown.
A subsequent [definition/evaluation split](research/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-abstract-split/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-abstract-split-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks all four symbolic-port and current-port definition/evaluation stages.
The source-zero composition and iPRA endpoint remain separate; the successful
generic provider is a stage from an otherwise failed earlier attempt.
A [factored endpoint attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-final-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-final-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the direct 32-coordinate expression's source-zero equivalence in the
intended models. The following endpoint stage failed on a zero-predicate type
mismatch; its `sorryAx` reports are not proof evidence, and its final
postflight did not run. An [unfolding retry](research/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-final-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign32-direct-ipra-final-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the direct `Fin 32` expression's polynomial bridge and native,
standard and translated-standard iPRA proof-existence equivalences. It uses
the successful source-zero stage of the preceding otherwise-failed run and
excludes its failed endpoint stage. This does not establish a Lean degree
bound, a binding to the external JSON or numerical coefficients, or a
sextic-to-nine-variable Jones endpoint; inherited provider caveats apply.
An external [31-witness sextic candidate](research/nine-sharing-sign31-sextic-candidate/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-sextic-frontier-independent/README.md),
eliminates the `f2` cut from the 32-witness AST while preserving exact degree 6.
An [independently audited Lean source draft](research/nine-sharing-sign31-sextic-lean-draft/README.md)
sets out the corresponding syntax and lift/drop obligations, without a Lean
check. A [syntax and squares client](research/nine-sharing-sign31-seed-squares-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-seed-squares-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that the symbolic-port sign31 seed vanishes exactly when its 22
residuals vanish in the intended models. A [first-cut client](research/nine-sharing-sign31-first-cut-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-first-cut-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the eliminated square residual and the two affected row identities;
it records five unused-variable warnings. A [remaining-cuts client](research/nine-sharing-sign31-remaining-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-remaining-cuts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks q3 and the other 19 residual identities and row positions, with 37
unused-binder or unused-simp warnings. A [directional zero bridge](research/nine-sharing-sign31-zero-bridge-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-zero-bridge-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks both the natural lift and drop implications for the same arbitrary
symbolic ports in IΣ₁ models. A [seed iff client](research/nine-sharing-sign31-seed-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-seed-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks pointwise lift equivalence and same-input existential equivalence for
arbitrary common ports without a beta cast. A [current-source client](research/nine-sharing-sign31-current-source-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-current-source-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks current-port seed zero with leading input 1 and 31 natural witnesses
iff current source zero in the intended models. The direct iPRA endpoint
remains separate. A [direct endpoint attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign31-direct-ipra-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-direct-ipra-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
hit the interpreter memory limit in its definition-only first stage; no
object, endpoint or final postflight was produced. The internal cause is
unknown. A [narrow-import retry](research/nine-sharing-sign31-direct-ipra-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-direct-ipra-retry01-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks five expression, evaluation and source-zero stages, but its sixth
endpoint stage again hits the interpreter memory limit. Its exact internal
trigger is unknown, and no endpoint object or final postflight was produced.
A [four-stage endpoint retry](research/nine-sharing-sign31-direct-ipra-final-retry/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-direct-ipra-final-retry-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the direct `Fin 31` expression's polynomial bridge and native,
standard and translated-standard iPRA proof-existence equivalences. It
authenticates the five successful stages of the otherwise-failed predecessor
and excludes that predecessor's failed endpoint. Lean degree, external JSON
and numerical coefficient bindings remain separate. The
quartic Jones theorem does not apply directly to it. An external
[28-witness sextic candidate](research/nine-sharing-sign28-sextic-candidate/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-sextic-frontier-independent/README.md),
removes three more positive cuts with exact degree 6. An [independently
reviewed source-only bridge draft](research/nine-sharing-sign28-sextic-lean-draft/README.md)
sets out the three-coordinate lift and drop and retains an explicit beta-cast
obligation. A [syntax and squares client](research/nine-sharing-sign28-seed-squares-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-seed-squares-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that the symbolic-port seed is zero iff its 19 residuals vanish in
the intended models. A [three-cut client](research/nine-sharing-sign28-first-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-first-cuts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the three inserted-cut zeros and five affected residual identities,
with the explicit beta cast where needed and 15 unused-binder/simp warnings.
A [remaining-cuts client](research/nine-sharing-sign28-remaining-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-remaining-cuts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks q3 and 13 unaffected residual identities, with 21 unused-binder/simp
warnings. A [directional zero bridge](research/nine-sharing-sign28-zero-bridge-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-zero-bridge-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks lift and drop implications in IΣ₁ models under the explicit beta-cast
hypothesis. A [seed iff client](research/nine-sharing-sign28-seed-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-seed-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks pointwise and same-input existential equivalence under the same
hypothesis. A [current-port specialization](research/nine-sharing-sign28-current-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-current-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
discharges that hypothesis for the current ports and checks both equivalences
in IΣ₁ models. A [source-composition attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign28-current-source-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-current-source-iff-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
hit the interpreter memory limit without producing an object or final
postflight; the internal trigger is unknown. A [factored retry](research/nine-sharing-sign28-current-source-iff-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-current-source-iff-retry01-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the cons-form conversion but its second stage again hits the memory
limit, with no source object or final postflight. The precise trigger remains
unknown. An [imports-only diagnostic](research/nine-sharing-sign28-source-import-diagnostic/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-source-import-diagnostic-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
compiles each provider alone and both together; it does not establish the
cause of the earlier exception or prove source equivalence. An [explicit
transitivity retry](research/nine-sharing-sign28-current-source-iff-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-current-source-iff-retry02-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
also hits the interpreter memory limit and produces no source object or final
postflight; its trigger is unknown. An [abstract-target transport](research/nine-sharing-sign28-abstract-target-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-abstract-target-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the 28-coordinate equivalence to an arbitrary target conditional on
the corresponding 31-coordinate equivalence. It does not supply the concrete
source equivalence. An [expression-evaluation client](research/nine-sharing-sign28-expression-eval-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-expression-eval-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the symbolic and current input-removal evaluations in four stages.
An [expression equivalence](research/nine-sharing-sign28-expression-equivalence-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-expression-equivalence-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks equality of zero existence for the direct 28- and 31-coordinate
expressions in the indicated IΣ₁ models. A [generic bridge-transport attempt](research/nine-sharing-generic-polynomial-bridge-transport-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-generic-polynomial-bridge-transport-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed on missing namespace and identifier elaboration; its `sorryAx` report
is not proof evidence, and it produced no object or final postflight. A
[namespace retry](research/nine-sharing-generic-polynomial-bridge-transport-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-generic-polynomial-bridge-transport-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the generic bridge transfer assuming both all-model zero equivalence
and the second expression's bridge. A [concrete sign28 specialization](research/nine-sharing-sign28-concrete-polynomial-bridge-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-concrete-polynomial-bridge-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks `PolynomialBridge` for the direct current-port 28-coordinate expression,
inheriting the sign31 source ancestry. A [native endpoint attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign28-endpoint-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-endpoint-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
hit the interpreter memory limit in its first stage, producing no endpoint
object or final postflight; the trigger is unknown. A [local-irreducibility retry](research/nine-sharing-sign28-endpoint-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign28-endpoint-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks native, standard and translated-standard iPRA proof-existence
equivalences for the direct current-port `Fin 28` expression. The sign31
source ancestry is inherited; Lean degree, external JSON and numerical
coefficient bindings, and a sextic-to-nine-variable Jones theorem remain
separate. The
source composition and
iPRA endpoint likewise remain to be checked in Lean. A further external
[27-witness sextic candidate](research/nine-sharing-sign27-sextic-normalized-root-candidate/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-sextic-root-independent/README.md),
normalizes a positive norm root and removes its product coordinate. Its
positive-root, predecessor and embedding bridge remains unproved in Lean.
An [independently reviewed source plan](research/nine-sharing-sign27-lean-bridge-plan/README.md)
and [syntax draft](research/nine-sharing-sign27-sextic-lean-draft/README.md)
make the predecessor and two coordinate-recovery obligations explicit; they
are not Lean-checked proofs. A [syntax and squares client](research/nine-sharing-sign27-seed-squares-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-seed-squares-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic seed zero iff all 18 residuals vanish in the intended models.
Its source-review receipt retains the historical appended-plan-link replay
caveat. An [abstract root-recovery client](research/nine-sharing-sign27-root-recovery-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-root-recovery-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the predecessor and product reconstruction from an explicit embedded
root equation. A [seed-root extraction client](research/nine-sharing-sign27-seed-root-recovery-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-seed-root-recovery-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that a symbolic sign28 seed zero supplies a predecessor and both
reconstruction equalities. A [first-cut attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign27-first-cut-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-first-cut-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
left a numeral-cast equality unsolved. Its `sorryAx` report is not proof
evidence; no object or final postflight was produced. A [numeral-cast retry](research/nine-sharing-sign27-first-cut-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-first-cut-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the deleted norm zero and first common residual identity, with two
linter warnings. An [affected-cuts client](research/nine-sharing-sign27-affected-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-affected-cuts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the three remaining residuals affected by the root substitution, with
six linter warnings. A [remaining-cuts client](research/nine-sharing-sign27-remaining-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-remaining-cuts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the 14 unaffected residual identities, with 20 linter warnings. The
[full directional zero bridge](research/nine-sharing-sign27-zero-bridge-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-zero-bridge-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks both lift and reverse directions in the indicated IΣ₁ models, with
the reverse direction constructing the predecessor and recovery equations.
A [same-input existential client](research/nine-sharing-sign27-seed-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-seed-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks seed zero-existence equivalence for the same symbolic ports and input.
An [expression-evaluation client](research/nine-sharing-sign27-expression-eval-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-expression-eval-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks abstract and current input-removal evaluations in four stages. The
[direct-expression equivalence](research/nine-sharing-sign27-expression-equivalence-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-expression-equivalence-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks zero-existence equivalence with the direct sign28 expression in the
indicated models, with one unused-section warning. A [concrete polynomial
bridge](research/nine-sharing-sign27-concrete-polynomial-bridge-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-concrete-polynomial-bridge-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
transports the sign28 bridge to the direct sign27 expression. The iPRA
endpoint is checked by a [direct endpoint client](research/nine-sharing-sign27-endpoint-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-endpoint-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md):
native, standard and translated-standard iPRA proof-existence equivalences
hold for the symbolic current-port `Fin 27` expression. The sign28/sign31
source ancestry and partial-provider caveats are inherited; Lean degree,
external JSON and numerical coefficient bindings, and a sextic-to-nine-variable
Jones theorem remain separate.
A further external [26-witness sextic candidate](research/nine-sharing-sign26-sextic-gap-congruence-candidate/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-sextic-gap-independent/README.md),
eliminates the gap witness using congruence modulo the q3 residual. Its
conditional positivity and natural reconstruction argument is not yet a Lean
zero bridge or iPRA endpoint; the existing quartic Jones theorem does not
apply directly. An [independently reviewed bridge plan](research/nine-sharing-sign26-lean-bridge-plan/README.md)
retains the q3 congruence, Nt positivity, port-cast and natural-representation
obligations; it is not a Lean proof. An [independently reviewed syntax and
bridge draft](research/nine-sharing-sign26-sextic-lean-draft/README.md) makes
the inserted gap representative and hypotheses explicit; its targets remain
unproved. A [syntax and squares client](research/nine-sharing-sign26-seed-squares-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-seed-squares-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic seed zero iff its 17 residuals vanish in the indicated models,
retaining the historical appended-plan-link replay caveat. An [ordered-ring
attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign26-ordered-ring-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-ordered-ring-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed because the `ring` tactic was unavailable; its `sorryAx` reports are
not proof evidence and no object or final postflight was produced. An
[explicit-import retry](research/nine-sharing-sign26-ordered-ring-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-ordered-ring-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the generic congruence and gap nonnegativity under explicit ordered-ring
hypotheses, with two linter warnings. A [zero-extraction client](research/nine-sharing-sign26-zero-extraction-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-zero-extraction-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that symbolic sign26 seed zero supplies the q3 and Nt ring equations.
A [conditional gap-representative client](research/nine-sharing-sign26-gap-representative-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-gap-representative-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks natural gap representability assuming beta≥1 and the embedded Nt
equation. A [seed-to-gap composition](research/nine-sharing-sign26-seed-gap-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-seed-gap-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks representability from seed zero under explicit beta/K port casts and
beta≥1. A [current-hypotheses client](research/nine-sharing-sign26-current-hypotheses-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-current-hypotheses-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks those casts and beta positivity at the current ports. A [current
seed-gap specialization](research/nine-sharing-sign26-current-seed-gap-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-current-seed-gap-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the represented gap from current-port seed zero; the full model bridge
remains separate. A [congruence-cuts client](research/nine-sharing-sign26-congruence-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-congruence-cuts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the removed gap row and the Tau/Inner transfers under explicit gap and
q3 equalities, with two linter warnings. A [remaining-cuts client](research/nine-sharing-sign26-remaining-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-remaining-cuts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the other 14 residual transfers, with 24 linter warnings. The assembled
zero bridge was attempted in a [three-stage client](research/nine-sharing-sign26-zero-bridge-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-zero-bridge-failure-frontier-independent/README.md):
the coordinate and conditional forward stages compiled, but the reverse stage
hit the 200,000-heartbeat limit, with no reverse object or final postflight.
A [factored reverse retry](research/nine-sharing-sign26-zero-bridge-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-zero-bridge-retry01-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
again compiled the earlier stages but failed its new helper on unreduced
finite-vector applications; its `sorryAx` report is not proof evidence, and
the final reverse stage and postflight did not run. A [simplification retry](research/nine-sharing-sign26-zero-bridge-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-zero-bridge-retry02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks both zero implications: forward assumes beta/K casts and beta≥1,
while reverse needs no additional port assumptions beyond IΣ₁. It records two
unused-section warnings. Same-input equivalence and current-port
specialization follow in stages. A [same-input seed iff](research/nine-sharing-sign26-seed-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-seed-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks existential equivalence under the same explicit port casts and beta
bound. A [current-port specialization](research/nine-sharing-sign26-current-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-current-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
discharges these hypotheses for the fixed ports and checks same-input seed
equivalence. An [expression-evaluation client](research/nine-sharing-sign26-expression-eval-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-expression-eval-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks abstract and current input-removal evaluations in four stages; no
source bridge or endpoint follows from those lemmas alone. A [direct-expression
equivalence](research/nine-sharing-sign26-expression-equivalence-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-expression-equivalence-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks zero-existence equivalence with the direct sign27 expression in the
indicated models, with one unused-section warning. A [concrete polynomial
bridge](research/nine-sharing-sign26-concrete-polynomial-bridge-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-concrete-polynomial-bridge-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
transports the sign27 bridge to the direct sign26 expression. The iPRA
endpoint remains separate, with earlier source ancestry inherited. A [native
endpoint attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign26-endpoint-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-endpoint-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
hit the interpreter memory limit in its first stage, producing no endpoint
object or final postflight; the trigger is unknown. A [restricted q3-remainder survey](research/nine-sharing-sign26-q3-single-cut-survey/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-q3-single-cut-independent/README.md),
examines ten direct cuts under one formal monic reduction rule; it is not a
semantic lower bound or an obstruction to other encodings.
A [local Nt divisibility obstruction](research/nine-sharing-sign26-nt-divisibility-local-obstruction/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign26-nt-divisibility-independent/README.md),
shows that dropping Nt and its sole v4 coordinate loses a condition in a
local parameter family. It does not constrain solutions of the full system
or establish a witness-count lower bound.
A [one-step survey](research/nine-sharing-sign27-single-positive-cut-survey/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-single-cut-independent/README.md),
checks the syntactic-degree effect of the seven directly eligible positive
cuts in that candidate. It excludes other reductions and is not a lower bound.
A [local gap/Nt obstruction](research/nine-sharing-sign27-gap-nt-local-obstruction/README.md),
[independently checked](reviews/nine-sharing-sign27-gap-nt-independent/README.md),
shows that those two equations and basic nonnegativity alone do not force the
coordinate reconstructed by a prospective gap elimination to be natural.
It does not constrain the full system or prove a witness-count lower bound.
A [restricted survey](research/nine-sharing-sign31-sextic-positive-cut-survey/README.md),
[independently reviewed](reviews/nine-sharing-sign31-positive-cut-survey-frontier-independent/README.md),
enumerates the sequential positive-cut substitutions that preserve
syntactic residual degree at most three. Its maximum of three further cuts
is not a semantic lower bound or a constraint on different encodings.
A [single-cut syntactic substitution survey](research/nine-sharing-sign33-single-cut-substitution-survey/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-single-cut-survey-frontier-independent/README.md),
finds a degree-3 or degree-4 residual in each of the 19 single-cut substitutions
into this residual list. This describes syntax only; it does not exclude
cancellation, other encodings, multiple changes or a 32-witness quartic.

A [nine-natural-coordinate Jones expression built from the 38-witness seed](research/nine-sharing-norm38-ipra-endpoint-check/README.md)
now has [independently audited Lean endpoints](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-ipra-endpoint-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md)
for the native and standard iPRA mapped-refutation equivalences and the
standard mapped Gödel–Gentzen consistency equivalence. The checked expression
is symbolic, `ExecutableJones.expression (SharingSeed38.seed
CurrentWindowCarry2011.ports) 0`; the coordinates range over the naturals,
including zero. This does not yet bind an exported literal equation or PDF,
or establish the final expression's operation count or exact degree. The retained
initial-agreement ancestor has successful Lean child exits but no captured
numeric outer exit; its inherited provider and point-observation limits remain.
An [external degree survey](research/nine-seed38-jones-metric-survey/README.md),
[independently reviewed by manual transcription and affine propagation](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-metric-transcription-frontier-independent/README.md),
gives the conservative upper bound `291404·5^39 + 860` for that symbolic
nine-variable expression. This is not a Lean degree theorem, an exact degree,
an operation count, or a literal-export binding.
A [compact named-formula draft](research/nine-seed38-jones-named-formula-draft/FORMULA.md)
has passed an [independent manual transcription review](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-named-formula-frontier-independent/README.md).
A [Lean evaluation specialization](research/nine-seed38-jones-named-formula-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-named-formula-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the associated symbolic ring evaluation. The human document has not
been machine-bound to the checked expression.
A [45-named-node macro-DAG prototype](research/nine-seed38-jones-compact-macro-prototype/README.md)
has passed an [independent manual recipe audit](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-compact-macro-frontier-independent/README.md).
This counts named nodes, not arithmetic operations; its semantics and JSON
serialization are not yet proved equal to the checked Lean expression.
A [source-only typed macro evaluator and quoted recipe draft](research/nine-seed38-jones-compact-lean-draft/README.md)
defines open well-scopedness and semantic targets; it does not prove them.
An [independent source comparison](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-compact-lean-draft-frontier-independent/README.md)
matches the 45 generated Lean node trees to the JSON, but is not a verified
parser, serializer or semantic equality proof.
A [macro-language-only Lean attempt](research/nine-seed38-jones-compact-language-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-compact-language-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed to parse the reserved binder name `prefix` and produced no object; it
did not test the generated recipe or establish semantic equality.
A [binder-rename retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-compact-language-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-compact-language-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then compiled the language and evaluator definitions. It did not compile the
generated recipe or prove well-scopedness, evaluation equality or JSON binding.
A [generated-recipe elaboration client](research/nine-seed38-jones-compact-recipe-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-compact-recipe-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
subsequently compiled the 45-node data and two proposition definitions. Those
propositions are not proved, and the JSON translator remains unverified.
A [scope-proof attempt](research/nine-seed38-jones-compact-scoped-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-compact-scoped-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed because Lean could not synthesize a `Decidable` instance for the
named target. Its `sorryAx` report is from the failed declaration;
no scopedness proof or semantic equality was produced.
A [target-unfolding retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-compact-scoped-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-compact-scoped-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then proved the Lean recipe's Boolean scope property without axioms. This is
not a semantic equality or verified JSON binding.
[Generic macro evaluation cuts](research/nine-seed38-jones-compact-semantics-cut-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-compact-semantics-cut-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
now check earlier/last DAG-node lookups and the symbolic range/star sum
evaluation rules. They do not yet prove the concrete recipe's meaning.
A [first concrete node attempt](research/nine-seed38-jones-compact-first-node-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-compact-first-node-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
hit the sampled 4-GiB session-RSS stop with an empty compile log; it produced
no theorem. The precise cause of the memory use remains unknown.
A [named-prefix build](research/nine-seed38-jones-named-prefix-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-named-prefix-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checked that the original Lean recipe equals the factored `d45` and
proved the value of the short `d1` prefix. A [preservation client](research/nine-seed38-jones-first-preservation-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-first-preservation-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks the first entry of the complete recipe. Full recipe semantic
equality remains open.
A [three-prefix attempt](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group01-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group01-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed on `Fin.last` versus numeral-index rewrites for two entries. The first
declaration had a clean report, but the failed module produced no object or
successful final survey.
A [targeted index retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group01-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group01-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checked the short-prefix values for `B`, `q` and `N`. Their preservation
to full-recipe entries and the full semantic equality remain separate.
A [second staged prefix group](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group02-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group02-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the short-prefix carry and `e`, `ell`, `lambda` identities without
enumerating their symbolic sums. Full-recipe preservation and semantic
equality remain open.
A [third staged prefix group](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group03-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group03-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the short-prefix carries and `g`, `theta`, `G` identities while
keeping the earlier sums opaque. Full-recipe semantic equality remains open.
A [fourth prefix group](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group04-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group04-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks seven short-prefix input and shifted-input values without unfolding
the prior rank expression. Full-recipe semantic equality remains open.
A [fifth preservation client](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group05-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group05-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the `q` and `N` values at prefix `d10`; it does not yet identify the
rank node or complete the recipe semantics.
A [sixth staged preservation client](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group06-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-group06-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
carries seven further opaque values to `d10`; the rank-node evaluation and
full recipe semantics remain open.
A [rank-node attempt](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-rank-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-rank-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed on the raw evaluator `rfl`; its second stage did not run and the
failed declaration's `sorryAx` is not proof evidence.
A [structural-evaluator retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-rank-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-rank-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checked the symbolic `d11` rank expression and its prefix carry
substitutions. Its identification with `rankCode` and full recipe semantics
remain separate.
A [generic rank-code link](research/nine-seed38-jones-rankcode-link-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-rankcode-link-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the `rankRaw`/`rankCode` identity after the explicit `B=b^5`
substitution. The actual-prefix substitution and full semantics remain
separate.
A [prefix power client](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-b-power-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-b-power-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks `B=b^5` at the actual `d10` prefix. Its composition with the rank
identity and the full recipe semantics remain separate.
A [rank composition attempt](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-rank-composition-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-rank-composition-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed on a `Fin` zero-index presentation mismatch after the substantive
rewrites. It produced no object; the failed declaration's `sorryAx` is not
proof evidence.
A [zero-index retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-rank-composition-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-rank-composition-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then identifies `d11` with `rankCode` applied to symbolic `d10` entries.
Replacing those entries by their concrete symbolic values and full recipe
semantics remain separate.
A [symbolic-value client](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-rank-symbolic-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-rank-symbolic-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then substitutes the checked short-prefix values into that `d11` rank
identity, retaining the large sums symbolically. Full-recipe semantic equality
remains open.
A [live-entry carry build](research/nine-seed38-jones-live18-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-live18-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks eleven prior values preserved through `d18`; the twelfth live input is
already the last node there. These are carry equalities only.
A [dependent-node client](research/nine-seed38-jones-dependent01-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-dependent01-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the short-prefix `U` and `Y` equations using the `d18` live values.
Later nodes and full recipe semantics remain separate.
A [second dependent-node build](research/nine-seed38-jones-dependent02-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-dependent02-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the short-prefix `M`, `P`, and `K` equations and their required carries.
Later nodes and full recipe semantics remain separate.
A [third dependent-node build](research/nine-seed38-jones-dependent03-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-dependent03-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the short-prefix `A`, `Bprime`, `W`, and `C` equations and their
required carries. Later nodes and full recipe semantics remain separate.
A [fourth dependent-node build](research/nine-seed38-jones-dependent04-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-dependent04-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the short-prefix `J`, `Q`, and `D` equations and their required carries.
Later nodes and full recipe semantics remain separate.
A [fifth dependent-node build](research/nine-seed38-jones-dependent05-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-dependent05-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the short-prefix `E` and `F` equations and their required carries. Later
nodes and full recipe semantics remain separate.
A [sixth dependent-node build](research/nine-seed38-jones-dependent06-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-dependent06-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the short-prefix `Gprime` and `H` equations and their required carries.
Later nodes and full recipe semantics remain separate.
A [seventh dependent-node build](research/nine-seed38-jones-dependent07-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-dependent07-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the short-prefix `I`, `A0`, `A1`, `V0`, and `V1` equations and their
required carries. Later nodes and full recipe semantics remain separate.
The [final local-node build](research/nine-seed38-jones-dependent08-staged-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-dependent08-staged-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the immediate-prefix equations for nodes `d40` through `d45`. Carrying
their dependencies and composing the full-recipe semantic equality remain
separate, as does binding the human-readable text to Lean.
A [live-carry inventory](research/nine-seed38-jones-live-carry-inventory/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-live-carry-inventory-frontier-independent/README.md),
counts 89 distinct named consumer edges and 403 single-snoc steps for a fresh
per-entry carry strategy. These are external planning counts, not a Lean proof,
minimality result or arithmetic operation count; existing carry lemmas may
reduce the work.
A [typed prefix-embedding client](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-embedding-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-embedding-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks generic lookup preservation along a snoc path and a two-snoc example.
A [recipe-specific client](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-path18-20-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-path18-20-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the path from `d18` to `d20` and preservation of every `d18` lookup.
A [longer path client](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-path18-27-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-path18-27-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
extends that preservation to `d27`. A [further path client](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-path18-34-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-path18-34-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
extends it to `d34`. Its first no-Lean preflight failed because it selected the
output object as provider; the corrected provider survey passed review. Further
paths and full semantic composition remain separate. A [next path client](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-path18-41-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-path18-41-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
extends this lookup preservation to `d41`; it does not establish the full
recipe equality. The [final path client](research/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-path18-45-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-prefix-path18-45-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
extends preservation to `d45` and the checked recipe for every `d18` entry.
Later-born values and full semantic composition remain separate.
A [later checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint27-34-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint27-34-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the `d27` to `d34` path, preservation of every `d27` lookup and one
concrete `phi` substitution. Further substitutions and full semantics remain
separate. A [subsequent checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint34-41-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint34-41-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the `d34` to `d41` path, every `d34` lookup and the symbolic `N` value
at `d41`. A [final checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint41-45-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint41-45-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks `d41` to `d45` and recipe lookup preservation, plus symbolic `g` and `G`
values at `d41`. Full semantic composition remains separate.
A [U/Y checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint20-27-uy-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint20-27-uy-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `U` and `Y` values at `d27` using a shared `d20` to `d27`
path. An [M checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint21-27-m-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint21-27-m-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks `M` at `d27` as the opaque `d11` rank times symbolic `Y`. Further
dependency-ordered substitutions remain separate. An [A checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint24-27-a-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint24-27-a-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the symbolic `A` value at `d27` using `M` and `U` while retaining the
rank opaquely. A [J checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint28-41-j-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint28-41-j-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `J` at `d28` and `d41` with the rank opaque. Full semantic
composition remains separate. A [C checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint26-27-c-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint26-27-c-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `Bprime`, `W` and `C` using the earlier values, keeping rank
and sums opaque. A [Q checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint29-41-q-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint29-41-q-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `Q` at `d29` and `d41` while keeping rank opaque. Full semantic
composition remains separate. A [D checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint30-41-d-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint30-41-d-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `D` at `d30` and `d41` with rank opaque. Full semantic
composition remains separate. An [E checkpoint attempt](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint31-41-e-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint31-41-e-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed on an unqualified `prefix_i` name and an unsolved goal; it produced no
object, did not run its second stage or final postflight, and its `sorryAx`
reports are not proof evidence. A [qualification-only retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint31-41-e-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint31-41-e-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks symbolic `E` at `d31` and `d41`, keeping rank opaque. Full semantic
composition remains separate. An [F checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint32-41-f-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint32-41-f-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `F` at `d32` and `d41` with rank opaque. Full semantic
composition remains separate. A [Gprime checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint33-41-gprime-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint33-41-gprime-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `Gprime` at `d33` and `d41` with rank opaque. Full semantic
composition remains separate. An [H checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint34-41-h-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint34-41-h-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `H` at `d34` and `d41` with rank opaque. Full semantic
composition remains separate. An [I checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint35-41-i-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint35-41-i-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `I` at `d35` and `d41` with rank opaque. Full semantic
composition remains separate. A [P/K checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint22-23-pk-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint22-23-pk-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic local `P` and `K` with rank opaque. Further substitutions and
full semantic composition remain separate. An [A0 checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint36-41-a0-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint36-41-a0-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `A0` at `d36` and `d41` with rank opaque. Full semantic
composition remains separate. An [A1 checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint37-41-a1-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint37-41-a1-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `A1` at `d37` and `d41` with rank opaque. Its independent
prelaunch provider replay was interrupted; the passing preflight was
author-observed. Full semantic composition remains separate.
A [V0/V1 checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint38-39-v-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint38-39-v-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks both symbolic values with rank opaque. The interrupted A1 independent
preflight and the earlier uncaptured outer exit remain inherited limitations;
full semantic composition remains separate.
A [Bd/Cd checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint40-41-bdcd-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint40-41-bdcd-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the two symbolic values at `d41`, retaining the inherited A1
interrupted-preflight and earlier outer-exit caveats. Full semantic composition
remains separate. A [Dm checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint42-dm-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint42-dm-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `Dm` at `d42` under the same inherited caveats; final semantic
composition remains separate. A [Kc checkpoint client](research/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint43-kc-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-checkpoint43-kc-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic `Kc` at `d43` under those caveats; final semantic composition
remains separate. A [terminal-cut attempt](research/nine-seed38-jones-terminal-cut-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-terminal-cut-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
compiled the `T` stage but failed the output stage on a `d44` rewrite mismatch.
Its `sorryAx` reports are not proof evidence; the final postflight did not run.
An [explicit-snoc retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-terminal-cut-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-terminal-cut-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks recipe output in seven unevaluated cuts, retaining inherited A1
and initial-exit caveats. Named-formula and JSON equality remain separate.
A [RingVars/Bd binding attempt](research/nine-seed38-jones-vars-bd-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-vars-bd-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed when `rfl` did not close the expanded Bd/RingVars equality; no object or
final postflight was produced, and its `sorryAx` report is not proof evidence.
A [targeted retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-vars-bd-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-vars-bd-retry01-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
still failed with casted integer versus ring-one expressions. It produced no
object or final postflight; its `sorryAx` report is not proof evidence. A
[cast-one retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-vars-bd-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-vars-bd-retry02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the symbolic Bd/dvsr binding with rank opaque and inherited A1 and
initial-exit caveats. A [Cd binding client](research/nine-seed38-jones-vars-cd-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-vars-cd-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks symbolic Cd/dvnd under the same caveats; the named-formula equality
remains separate. A [radicand binding client](research/nine-seed38-jones-vars-rad-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-vars-rad-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the A0/A1-to-RingVars radicand bindings under the same caveats; the
named-formula equality remains separate. A [weight binding client](research/nine-seed38-jones-vars-wt-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-vars-wt-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks V0/V1 against the RingVars weights under those caveats; the final
named-formula equality remains separate. A [margin and Kc binding client](research/nine-seed38-jones-vars-kc-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-vars-kc-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks Dm against the RingVars margin and Kc against `dvsr*(2*marg-1)`;
the rank remains opaque, and the inherited A1 and initial-exit caveats apply.
A [recipe-output composition](research/nine-seed38-jones-vars-output-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-vars-output-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks equality with `RefinedPair.value` of the symbolic RingVars under those
same caveats. Matching the opaque rank to the named evaluator and linking the
JSON remain separate. A [named-evaluator equality attempt](research/nine-seed38-jones-named-equality-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-named-equality-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed on an unsolved tuple equality involving concrete `Fin.cons` indices
and exponent additions. Its error-containing `sorryAx` report is not proof
evidence; the final equality stage and postflight did not run. A
[finite-index retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-named-equality-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-named-equality-retry01-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed earlier on dependent-codomain inference for its first `Fin.cons`
helper; neither the tuple theorem nor the final equality was established. A
[constant-family retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-named-equality-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-named-equality-retry02-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
elaborated the helpers but still left the tuple equality unsolved; the final
equality and postflight did not run. A [definitional-normalization retry](research/nine-seed38-jones-named-equality-retry03/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-seed38-jones-named-equality-retry03-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks equality of the Lean compact recipe and named expression
evaluation over commutative rings and `Fin 9` valuations. One
`unnecessarySeqFocus` warning and the inherited provider caveats remain; this
does not bind serialized JSON or human-readable text to Lean.
An [independently reviewed composition plan](research/nine-seed38-jones-semantic-composition-plan/README.md)
describes checkpoint paths and substitution in dependency order; it is a plan,
not a Lean proof or evidence that the complete composition fits the resource
limits.

The lower-degree P13 and P14 expressions have a [captured-provider replay](research/p72-p13-p14-captured-endpoint-replay/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/p72-p13-p14-captured-endpoint-independent/README.md),
of their positive-model bridges and native, standard and equality-only iPRA
mapped-refutation endpoints. Their externally compared graphs have 13 and 14
positive variables and use 98 and 101 operations, respectively, counting six
derived fixed integers as precomputed leaves. An external [fixed-byte degree
certificate](research/p72-fixed-byte-degree-certificate/README.md), [independently
audited](reviews/p72-fixed-byte-degree-independent/README.md), establishes
exact degrees 28 and 24 using exact residual bounds and modular lower witnesses.
The replay does not parse the graph/files in Lean. The [declared compact-port
paths](research/p72-compact-port-path-restore/README.md) have been restored from
hash-matched source44 payloads. A [translated-consistency client](research/p72-p13-p14-translated-captured/README.md),
[independently reviewed](reviews/p72-p13-p14-translated-captured-independent/README.md),
checks both mapped Gödel–Gentzen formulations on that same provider. It is a
captured provider, not a fresh current-base closure.
A subsequent [681-module current-base source extension](research/p72-current-base-extension-build-attempt02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/p72-current-base-extension-attempt02-independent/README.md),
completed with actual outer exit 0 and checked both P13 and P14 native,
standard and equality-only mapped-refutation endpoints. Its existing current14
base and package-transitive/dynamic ancestry retain their stated limits; the
fixed-byte graph and degree checks are separate. A [fresh-provider translated
client](research/p72-p13-p14-translated-fresh/README.md), [independently
audited](reviews/p72-p13-p14-translated-fresh-terminal-independent/README.md),
also checks both mapped Gödel–Gentzen consistency equivalences on this source
extension. A [further Lean client](research/p72-byte-recipe-endpoints-fresh/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/p72-byte-recipe-endpoints-fresh-terminal-independent/README.md),
states both equivalences at the four decoded symbolic byte recipes. Comparing
those recipes with compressed file contents remains an external check.
A [source-declared closure survey](research/p72-current-base-source-survey/README.md)
identifies 681 additional project modules over the current14 base. A separate
[combined external-import probe](research/p72-current-base-external-import-probe/README.md),
[independently reviewed](reviews/p72-current-base-external-import-independent/README.md),
checks the three additional external imports alongside the current endpoint;
it does not build those 681 project modules or establish their compatibility.

The [14-variable, 67-operation, degree-106 candidate](research/fourteen-normalized-product106/README.md)
has a focused [historical-provider check](research/normalized106-historical-endpoint-client/README.md)
of its all-model same-zero bridge and standard-iPRA derivability, together
with an independently reviewed [67-row source/graph comparison](research/normalized106-graph-syntax-binding/README.md).
The focused endpoint inherits historical package and transitive ancestry;
the numerical port-to-syntax binding and degree certificate remain separate
finite checks, and this is not a fresh current-base rebuild. The original
finite degree auditor rejects the later refactored Lean sources; an
[independently reproduced historical-input replay](research/normalized106-historical-finite-replay/README.md)
recovers its 67-operation/degree-106 certificate, without claiming a current-
source audit. A [16-module current-base source preflight](research/normalized106-current14-sixteen-source-preflight/README.md)
identifies a smaller route to rebuild the model bridge; a narrowly scoped
[combined-import probe](research/normalized106-direct-import-probe-a/README.md)
loaded the current endpoint with the five newly declared external modules. A
subsequent [current14-base extension](research/normalized106-fresh-base-model-build/README.md)
compiled all 16 modules and checked a same-zero bridge and standard-iPRA
derivability theorem for the 67/106 syntax. Its [independent audit](reviews/normalized106-fresh-base-model-independent/README.md)
checks the recorded direct inputs, outputs and process exits; external
package-transitive and dynamic ancestry remain inherited. The expression's
graph, degree and materialized-port correspondence are separate checks. A
[translated-consistency client](research/normalized106-fresh-base-translated/README.md)
also checks the equivalence with Foundation's mapped Gödel–Gentzen consistency
sentence on that same base.

The [71-operation/degree-80 and 72-operation/degree-68 integer sphere forms](research/fourteen-nonzero-spheres/README.md)
now have [current-base Lean clients](research/current2011-spheres-fresh-base/README.md)
proving same-valuation zero equivalences in all IΣ₁ models and standard-iPRA
derivability for each no-zero sentence. An [independent audit](reviews/current2011-spheres-fresh-base-independent/README.md)
checks both complete graph-to-source comparisons and establishes the exact
degrees for the recorded fixed bytes by upper bounds and modular univariate
lower bounds. The graph and degree checks are external; the coefficient bytes
are not yet fully bound to the Lean ports, and provider transitive and dynamic
ancestry remains inherited. A [separate client](research/current2011-spheres-fresh-translated/README.md)
checks both standard-iPRA equivalences with Foundation's mapped Gödel–Gentzen
consistency sentence; its [independent audit](reviews/current2011-spheres-fresh-translated-independent/README.md)
checks the recorded source and provider inputs.
A [71-operation, degree-60 sphere equation](research/current2011-ordinary71-60-candidate/README.md)
has a separately [audited graph and exact fixed-byte degree](reviews/current2011-ordinary71-60-graph-independent/README.md).
Its [generic ring theorem](research/current2011-ordinary71-60-generic/README.md),
[independently reviewed](reviews/current2011-ordinary71-60-generic-independent/README.md),
proves zero equivalence to the 67/94 value at the same positive coordinates
under the stated discrete-order and Pell facts. A [literal evaluator](research/current2011-ordinary71-60-literal/README.md)
and [all-model join](research/current2011-ordinary71-60-model-join/README.md)
connect this to the 67/94 syntax, and a [Lean endpoint](research/current2011-ordinary71-60-ipra/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/current2011-ordinary71-60-ipra-independent/README.md),
checks standard-iPRA derivability for both the mapped refutation negation and
mapped Gödel–Gentzen consistency. The degree and graph-to-file correspondence
remain external checks, and package-transitive ancestry remains inherited.
A [72-operation, degree-46 shear candidate](research/current2011-sheared72-46-candidate/README.md)
replaces the 71/60 auxiliary coordinate by `V+y`; its [independent graph audit](reviews/current2011-sheared72-46-independent/README.md)
checks the exact ring identity and fixed-byte degree. The substitution does
not automatically preserve strictly positive coordinates, and the all-model
zero bridge and iPRA endpoint remain open. It is not yet an established
consistency equation.
An [uncompiled proof draft](research/current2011-sheared72-46-proof-draft/README.md),
[statically reviewed](reviews/current2011-sheared72-46-proof-draft-independent/README.md),
isolates the needed ordinary-Pell residue and conditional order lemmas; it
does not derive their hypotheses from zeros or construct the model bridge.
A [70-operation, degree-84 equation](research/current2011-ordinary70-84-candidate/README.md)
has an independently checked [graph and fixed-byte degree](reviews/current2011-ordinary70-84-graph-independent/README.md).
Its [generic ring proof](research/current2011-ordinary70-84-generic/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/current2011-ordinary70-84-generic-independent/README.md),
establishes the same positive-coordinate zeros as 67/94 under its stated
ordered-ring facts. The [literal evaluator](research/current2011-ordinary70-84-literal/README.md)
and [model join](research/current2011-ordinary70-84-model-join/README.md) carry
this equality of zero predicates into every IΣ₁ model. A [Lean iPRA endpoint](research/current2011-ordinary70-84-ipra/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/current2011-ordinary70-84-ipra-independent/README.md),
checks both mapped-refutation and mapped Gödel–Gentzen consistency forms;
the graph degree and file correspondence remain external checks.
A [69-operation degree-84 graph](research/current2011-ordinary69-84-candidate/README.md)
uses `x(x−2) = (x−1)²−1` to compute exactly the same polynomial as the
70/84 form with one fewer operation. Its [independent audit](reviews/current2011-ordinary69-84-candidate-independent/README.md)
checks the full graph, fixed-byte degree and ring identity. A [Lean literal
client](research/current2011-ordinary69-84-literal/README.md) checks its
commutative-ring evaluation and exact value identity with 70/84; an [iPRA
