are not proven to be unique origins. Further unmatched producers could enlarge
it. A coherent rebuild or stronger audit is
needed; the hashes alone do not show mathematical unsoundness. A [source preflight](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuild-source-preflight/README.md)
matches all 114 candidate source hashes to retained producer pins and their
explicit import names to the captured headers. An [expanded preflight](reviews/ten-positive-80-expanded-rebuild-preflight/README.md)
does the same for all 122 provisional cone modules. Neither is a rebuild, and
source-byte matches do not establish unique producer origins. An [independent drift review](reviews/ten-positive-80-producer-drift-independent/README.md)
rechecks the eleven recorded mismatches and provisional reachability without
replaying Lean. A [guarded rebuild runner](research/ten-positive-80-coherent-rebuild/README.md)
was prepared for 122 modules but is superseded and held: a further historical
[comparison](reviews/ten-positive-80-ha87-producer-drift/README.md) found
eleven additional recorded direct-import differences, including one outside
Foundation object, and expanded the provisional cone to 127 modules.
An [independent HA87 review](reviews/ten-positive-80-ha87-drift-independent/README.md)
confirms the comparisons and reachability without replaying Lean. A
[127-module preflight](reviews/ten-positive-80-127-rebuild-preflight/README.md)
matches its sources and import names; it is not a rebuild or package-ancestry
authentication. The [independent v2 prelaunch review](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuild-v2-prelaunch-independent/README.md)
found memory-guard and diagnostic-matching blockers. A corrected v3 passed
[independent static review](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuild-v3-prelaunch-independent/README.md)
was followed by one guarded attempt. It stopped with outer exit 1 at stage 3
because the strict policy rejected three deprecation warnings; Lean itself
exited 0 for that stage. The retained diagnostic is being checked against a
historical baseline and a source modernization is under consideration. An
[independent stop audit](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuild-attempt01-independent/README.md)
confirms that the entire path-normalized warning log matches the historical
source-specific baseline; it does not turn the rejected run into a pass. Only
A [v4 retry](research/ten-positive-80-coherent-rebuild/V4.md) admits only the
exact pinned module/source/full normalized warning log and remains strict
elsewhere. An [independent v4 review](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuild-v4-prelaunch-independent/README.md)
checks its policy and outer wrapper. The fresh v4 attempt stopped with outer
exit 1 at stage 5 after accepting the exact stage-3 baseline; its three new
warnings match a separate historical source-specific baseline. An
[independent stop audit](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuild-attempt02-independent/README.md)
checks both stages and records that the attempt remains failed. A separately
reviewed [v5 retry policy](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuild-v5-prelaunch-independent/README.md)
allowed exactly those two source-specific complete warning logs. Its fresh
attempt stopped at stage 13, `ModelRatioBounds`, with outer exit 1 because of
four warnings; a source-specific historical comparison is in progress. No
attempt has passed. An [independent attempt audit](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuild-attempt03-independent/README.md)
checks the twelve preceding outputs, both accepted exceptions and the exact
historical stage-13 warning log without replaying Lean. A [v6 prelaunch audit](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuild-v6-prelaunch-independent/README.md)
checks a third exact module/source/full-log exception, with strict diagnostics
elsewhere. The fresh v6 attempt has now completed all 127 stages with final
PASS and outer exit 0, and an [independent post-run audit](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuild-attempt04-postrun-independent/README.md)
checks source/direct input snapshots, all outputs and headers, exact warning
exceptions and the four endpoint axiom reports without replaying Lean. The
fresh endpoint object matches the earlier endpoint hash; five of the 127
rebuilt objects differ. This scoped observed-cone replay does not authenticate
unrebuilt historical, package-transitive or dynamic ancestry or external files.
A [post-merge pin check](reviews/ten-positive-80-post-merge-source-check/README.md)
rechecked the 127 source pins and recorded replay/closure inputs after the
origin/main merge; it is not a new Lean replay or a full workspace diff.
A [fresh-union header rescan](reviews/ten-positive-80-coherent-rebuilt-closure/README.md)
reads all 1,765 selected project headers and reconstructs the same project and
outside reachability; an [independent audit](reviews/ten-positive-80-rebuilt-closure-independent/README.md)
checks the captured logs, objects and companions without replaying Lean. The
other 1,638 project source histories and outside-transitive ancestry remain
inherited, and the scan has no separately captured outer-process receipt.
The [historical warning inventory](reviews/ten-positive-80-warning-inventory/README.md)
identifies a further recorded policy at planned stage 13, but does not
pre-approve its output under the fresh provider. An [independent inventory review](reviews/ten-positive-80-warning-inventory-independent/README.md)
checks its source and evidence scope without treating it as a fresh-warning prediction.
Those
byte-matched records are not proven unique producer
origins. No replay is yet claimed. A
[staged-producer follow-up](reviews/ten-positive-80-staged-producer-audit/README.md)
matches seven previously unmatched fresh helper objects to multi-stage
receipts, while explicitly retaining the producer-time pin limitations and
24 remaining unmatched older batch objects. A
[base-10 decimal display](research/ten-positive-80-original-kernel-display/README.md)
uses ten distinct unknown names and the original coefficient tuple. Its
graph, payload and PDF checks are external, not a Lean file-binding theorem.
An [independent display audit](reviews/ten-positive-80-decimal-display-independent/README.md)
compares all rows against Lean source and JSON, the complete payloads and
extracted PDF text, without proving those file correspondences in Lean. A
[checked explicit-row theorem](research/ten-positive-80-explicit-rows-binding/README.md)
also identifies an explicitly listed 80-register Lean expression with the
graph output for arbitrary integer ports; the JSON/PDF and coefficient
correspondences in that packet remain external. An [independent row audit](reviews/ten-positive-80-displayed-rows-independent/README.md)
checks the arbitrary-port scope and selected graph object without replaying Lean.
A subsequent [explicit-row client](research/ten-positive-80-explicit-rows-binding/README.md#explicit-row-ipra-endpoints)
checks all four standard and equality-only iPRA derivability statements for
that same Lean row expression with the fixed symbolic ports and one positive
shift. An [independent source and receipt review](reviews/ten-positive-80-displayed-ipra-independent/README.md)
checks the four conclusions and selected direct providers without a Lean
replay. The client uses the selected 127-module rebuilt provider and inherits its
remaining ancestry limits; the printed constant and file correspondences are
still external.
A generic [scaled-from-unscaled Pell theorem](research/ten-positive-scaled-from-unscaled-prototype/README.md)
constructs the scaled interval at the same X,Y,r from explicit source
interval, power and size hypotheses. The generic result alone does not derive
its hypotheses from a 90 zero; later reverse graph and halt joins under
explicit symbolic assumptions are described below.
A [fresh scaled-witness join](research/ten-positive-scaled-witness-from-unscaled-prototype/README.md)
adds positive η′ and ζ′ for the scaled pair, explicitly distinct from the
unscaled source witnesses. This is still a conditional model theorem. A
[reverse-construction survey](reviews/ten-positive-90-to-80-reverse-plan/README.md)
maps the shared graph coordinates, distinct Pell bases, internal period lemma,
and outstanding carrier and provider obligations. A later [scaled strong-seed theorem](research/ten-positive-scaled-strong-seed-prototype/README.md)
uses the internal arbitrary-modulus Pell result to construct positive seed
indices and doubled coordinates for any positive c and τ. It is not yet
joined to a 90 zero or the remaining 80 carriers. A separate
[unbounded divisible-ordinate theorem](research/ten-positive-pell-ordinate-above-prototype/README.md)
chooses an internal divisible Pell index above any internal bound; it remains
to be used to establish the required carrier positivity. A
[strong-seed extension](research/ten-positive-scaled-strong-seed-above-prototype/README.md)
combines that bound with the doubled seed coordinates. An
[independent audit](reviews/ten-positive-scaled-strong-above-independent/README.md)
checks the selected receipts and notes the bound is on the index m, not
directly on i or f; it does not replay Lean. An
[independent receipt audit](reviews/ten-positive-scaled-reverse-and-halt-forward-independent/README.md)
checks the conditional forward halt, scaled-interval, fresh-witness and first
strong-seed results without replaying Lean.
A [generalized auxiliary-seed theorem](research/ten-positive-general-auxiliary-seed-prototype/README.md)
constructs positive auxiliary Pell witnesses for a supplied strong root under
explicit norm, divisibility and phase assumptions, including the essential
`c = ycoord(R,a)` identity. It does not itself assume a graph zero.
A [scaled auxiliary join](research/ten-positive-scaled-auxiliary-seed-prototype/README.md)
now combines the strong and auxiliary witnesses in one tuple and makes the
strong root f exceed any given internal bound. It retains explicit phase,
ordinate and positivity assumptions; graph and carrier binding remain open.
A [generic positive carrier quotient](research/ten-positive-scaled-kappa-generic-prototype/README.md)
derives the needed projection divisibility from `X=2^R` and `X<ycoord(R,a)`
without assuming a graph zero. Its hypotheses and the remaining transport
equation still need to be bound to the same source witnesses.
A [generic scaled offset](research/ten-positive-scaled-offset-generic-prototype/README.md)
constructs the natural μ² quotient of the first-Pell index residue without
an 80-zero hypothesis; it does not assert that quotient is positive.
A [reverse-start join](research/ten-positive-scaled-reverse-start-prototype/README.md)
combines the fresh scaled interval, positive first root, offset and projection
quotients at one X,Y,r under explicit unscaled source hypotheses. It still
does not establish those hypotheses from a 90 zero or a positive 80 valuation.
An [independent start audit](reviews/ten-positive-scaled-reverse-start-independent/README.md)
checks the shared tuple and distinguishes the unscaled `Y*(X+1)` Pell base
from the scaled `(μY)*(X+1)-1` base, without replaying Lean.
A [conditional positive carrier theorem](research/ten-positive-reverse-carrier-prototype/README.md)
uses the two quotients and an explicit `κ<f` bound to construct n>0 and prove
the signed carrier identity. It does not yet bind that identity to the actual
80 graph evaluation or prove its output zero. An [independent ingredient audit](reviews/ten-positive-reverse-carrier-ingredients-independent/README.md)
checks the selected receipts, assumptions and direct inputs without replaying Lean.
A [reverse cross identity](research/ten-positive-reverse-cross-prototype/README.md)
and [actual 80-output binding](research/ten-positive-80-reverse-output-parts-prototype/README.md)
show the selected output vanishes from explicit coherent scalar-part
equalities and the auxiliary norm. The positive valuation and those graph-part
equalities are still hypotheses to be constructed, not yet a reverse proof.
An [independent cross/output audit](reviews/ten-positive-reverse-cross-output-independent/README.md)
checks the selected receipts and exact hypothesis scope without replaying Lean.
A [coherent reverse-data join](research/ten-positive-scaled-reverse-data-prototype/README.md)
now assembles one fresh positive scaled tuple satisfying the first interval,
both quotient identities, strong/auxiliary norms and signed carrier parts,
under explicit unscaled interval, power, size and phase hypotheses. Its
transport inputs remain arbitrary; actual graph syntax and source-zero
binding were separate at that stage. An [independent reverse-data audit](reviews/ten-positive-scaled-reverse-data-independent/README.md)
checks the selected direct inputs and shared existential scope without
replaying Lean; I and ρ are not asserted positive.
A [conditional reverse-graph theorem](research/ten-positive-scaled-reverse-graph-prototype/README.md)
now binds the scalar parts to the actual selected 80 graph and constructs a
positive zero under explicit source interval, rank, power, size, phase and
transport hypotheses. It exports existence, not retained-coordinate equalities.
An [independent graph audit](reviews/ten-positive-scaled-reverse-graph-independent/README.md)
checks these premises and selected receipts without replaying Lean. Deriving
the hypotheses from an actual 90 zero remains open.
A [packing/seed adapter](research/ten-positive-packing-reverse-graph-prototype/README.md)
now derives the graph mask, rank, X, Y and transport bindings from one
coherent packing and seed tuple, yielding a positive 80 zero under its
explicit port, positivity, phase, power and size assumptions. This is an
author check; an [independent packing audit](reviews/ten-positive-packing-reverse-graph-independent/README.md)
checks its exact premises and direct providers without replaying Lean.
A [halt-to-80 join](research/ten-positive-packing-reverse-graph-prototype/HALT.md)
now obtains one packing and seed from the internal halt constructor and
derives the remaining phase and size assumptions, yielding a positive 80
zero under all four explicit model port equalities. In particular the kernel
port is the original symbolic `paramKernel rho`; this author check is not
yet an iPRA theorem or a binding of the distinct retained BFS payload. An
[independent halt-reverse audit](reviews/ten-positive-halt-reverse-graph-independent/README.md)
checks its selected providers and same packing/seed witness without replaying Lean.
A generic [positive ordinary-seed theorem](research/ten-positive-pell-positive-seed-prototype/README.md)
constructs the needed positive Pell coordinates from an explicit rank phase;
it has not yet been bound to the same source graph valuation.
A separate [receipt audit](reviews/direct-bfs-raw-column-positive-transport-independent/README.md)
records the coefficient-decoder import-pin gap and the positive transport
scope; it does not replay Lean.
A separate [canonical-bounds theorem](research/ten-positive-80-canonical-bounds-prototype/README.md)
proves `rank > 3*q` and `q^3 ∣ Y` under explicit beta and positivity bounds,
without assuming a graph zero. An [independent audit](reviews/ten-positive-80-carry-fresh-interval-independent/README.md)
checks nine selected carry and fresh-interval author receipts, without a Lean
replay. The full compact window, source graph zero and machine theorem are
not yet established.

A [signed-V orientation theorem](research/ten-positive-80-v-positive-prototype/README.md)
derives positivity of the 80 graph's signed `V` from an actual zero under
positive beta and low/high port bounds, without assuming beta oddness. The
rank phase follows under the symbolic fixed ports as described above; machine
decoding and the iPRA endpoint remain open.
After power recovery, an [unweighted projection](research/ten-positive-80-projection-product-prototype/UNWEIGHTED.md)
derives `H ∣ Kpos` directly from the Pell norm, without canceling `b` or the
power, and obtains a positive natural quotient under the same port bounds.
The [integral transport join](research/ten-positive-80-transport-integral-graph-prototype/README.md)
then derives a natural quotient for the actual signed transport under explicit
beta-size, oddness and low/high port assumptions, without assuming `Y` is
coprime to `b`. An [independent audit](reviews/ten-positive-80-integral-transport-independent/README.md)
checks the unweighted projection, cancellation and selected receipts without
replaying Lean. A [natural transport join](research/ten-positive-80-natural-transport-prototype/README.md)
uses the proven mask gap to recover the exact positive mask equation.
A [serialized-import preflight](research/ordinary12-sum-squares-endpoint-import-preflight/README.md)
and its [independent review](reviews/ordinary12-sum-squares-endpoint-import-preflight-independent/README.md)
find no member of the 425-module rebuild cone among the recorded selected-project
ancestors of the 94-operation model join. This does not authenticate inherited
package ancestry or replace a coherent compilation of the final client.

The earlier 94- and 95-operation compact graphs do not yet have a checked iPRA
endpoint. A separately checked
[generic positive-model adapter](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/positive-model-ipra-adapter-prototype/README.md)
was subsequently combined with the 90-operation graph using the guarded
rebuild described below. The symbolic coefficients are
also not formally bound to retained payload bytes or a PDF. In particular,
as documented in the [display boundary](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/positive-shift-display-boundary/README.md),
`equation12.pdf` displays a separate 26-variable graph, and `equation.pdf`
displays the separate 14-variable equation. The adapter's positive shift
substitutes `x + 1`; a different same-named function substitutes `x - 1`.
For the first symbolic port, a [checked byte-recipe theorem](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/compact44-retained-beta-byte-prototype/README.md)
now identifies beta with a simple little-endian base-256 digit list; an
external check separately matches that list to the retained compressed
payload. For the MC port, a [guarded author build](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/compact44-low-byte-assembly-attempt2/README.md)
now proves that a closed 73,759-entry Lean byte recipe decodes to the
symbolic low mask. A separate [shifted-MF reconstruction](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/compact44-mf-byte-assembly-prototype/README.md)
proves that a closed byte recipe decodes to beta plus the symbolic high mask,
with beta added once. A [four-port Lean theorem](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/compact44-four-port-byte-assembly2/README.md)
now joins closed beta, K, MC and shifted-MF byte recipes to the exact symbolic
tuple. External pinned checks compare recipe transcriptions with the retained
parent payloads; Lean does not yet establish those file or gzip
correspondences. A separate [all-model client](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/compact44-byte-ports-model-iff-prototype/README.md)
uses those naturally decoded recipes in the generated 95-gate Lean expression
and proves shifted zero iff internal machine halt in each IΣ₁ model. This is
not yet an internal iPRA derivation; its [source and selected-provider review](reviews/compact44-byte-ports-model-iff-source-review/README.md)
is not a fresh replay or a full ancestry audit. The supplement's missing
binding and payloads have been [restored from exact manifest-pinned sources](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/supplement-pinned-restoration/README.md),
and external checks establish the parent-to-supplement byte correspondence.
The Lean file/gzip and JSON transcriptions and PDF correspondence remain open. The
[serialized-import inventory](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/compact12-ipra-object-import-prototype/README.md)
also supports the initial 59-module rebuild within its scoped namespace,
without certifying all other project or package ancestry. A guarded
[15-stage author prefix](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/compact12-ipra-incremental-rebuild-merged-batch03/README.md)
has since compiled, but a broader serialized-import reverse-impact scan
finds a [425-module endpoint ancestor cone](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/compact12-ipra-expanded-cone-preflight/README.md): the original 59 omissions plus
366 affected nine-provider objects. The first 15 rebuilt stages have
unresolved affected ancestors and must be treated as provisional until
those predecessors and the stages themselves are rebuilt coherently. The
source files for the enlarged cone are identifiable; their historical
source-to-object origins remain incompletely authenticated. A fresh,
guarded [expanded-cone build](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/compact12-ipra-expanded-cone-rebuild-stage420/README.md)
has rebuilt all 425 selected modules. A missing declaration in a
historical provider required a checked local source repair at stage 276;
further source repairs were required at later stages, and inherited provider
freshness remains open. At stage 64 a warning-bearing
attempt was rejected; the minimal unused-argument correction was separately
re-pinned and checked. Later unchanged theorem interfaces required three
separately source-pinned, exact-warning baselines, with further baselines at
stages 197, 224, 263, 270 and 272 to preserve existing interfaces and at stages
250–251, 266 and 268 for source-pinned style warnings. Stage 95's initial
three-minute attempt timed out; a separately guarded longer retry passed.
The final 90-operation client has since passed the guarded author join described
above. A separately checked
[conditional adapter](research/ordinary12-ipra-bridge/positive-model-ipra-standard-adapter-prototype/README.md)
also supplies standard and equality-only iPRA conclusions for any positive
all-model bridge; it has now been joined to the 90-operation compact bridge in a coherent
build.

A read-only [current-base source survey](research/nine-variable-current-base-source-survey/README.md)
identifies 995 shared modules and a 217-module extension for both nine-variable
endpoints and the check client; the base records and sources were rehashed.
This is not a Lean rebuild or a package-transitive compatibility check. A
[bounded external-dependency probe](research/nine-variable-external-matrix-preflight/README.md)
resolved direct seeds and visited 64 serialized headers, but 199 frontier
modules remain; Matrix compatibility and full package reachability are still
unresolved. Its independent audit also identifies process-cleanup requirements
for any extended traversal. A [revised runner review](reviews/nine-variable-header-resume-prelaunch-v3-independent/README.md)
records passing synthetic controls but an unresolved numeric-PID reuse risk;
a separately reviewed process-handle prototype is not production-ready. No
continuation traversal has been launched.

The newly merged [nine-variable endpoint](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAExplicitZFCFinal.lean)
states native, standard and equality-only iPRA equivalences without hypotheses;
the separate [CURRENT2011 nine-variable endpoint](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANineCurrent.lean)
does the same for a different equation. Both have now passed a
[104-stage incremental author-side replay](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-complete-author-ledger/README.md)
on a historical provider; the endpoint packets retain the 15 CURRENT2011 and
four ExplicitZFC named axiom reports. A separate guarded peer has now also
completed all 104 stages. Its [aggregate audit](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-complete-peer-ledger/README.md)
records matching endpoint outputs and named reports but 20 differing object
hashes and 31 differing direct-input pairs, so this is not an exact
full-chain paired replay or a complete inherited-ancestry audit.
A [lightweight quartic bridge](research/nine-current-lightweight-quartic-probe/README.md),
[independently reviewed](reviews/nine-current-lightweight-quartic-independent/README.md),
now checks on a captured source-built provider while avoiding an unrelated
current14-specific square-sum dependency. It is a generic IΣ₁-model bridge,
not yet a fresh nine-variable encoding or iPRA endpoint.
A subsequent [20-stage Jones model build](research/nine-current-lightweight-jones-probe/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-current-lightweight-jones-independent/README.md),
checks the generic nine-coordinate Jones correspondence over that provider.
The [concrete current-equation client](research/nine-current-lightweight-endpoint-retry/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-current-lightweight-endpoint-independent/README.md),
now checks native and standard iPRA mapped-refutation endpoints and the mapped
Gödel–Gentzen standard endpoint, with five explicit axiom reports. Its nine
coordinates range over naturals including zero; no literal graph, operation
count, degree or file semantics is established by this client. A [symbolic
ring evaluator](research/nine-current-symbolic-ring-generic/README.md) and
[readable equation](research/nine-current-symbolic-ring-generic/EQUATION.md)
make the finite-sum/power recipe explicit; their [review](reviews/nine-current-symbolic-ring-independent/README.md)
checks the recorded theorem and transcription without claiming a Lean
Markdown or filesystem interpretation. An [external structural count](research/nine-current-seed-count-diagnostic/README.md),
[independently checked](reviews/nine-current-seed-count-independent/README.md),
finds 8,446,685 nodes on each natural split and 16,893,385 quartic witnesses.
These are source-structure counts, not a Lean numerical equality or a count
of operations in the resulting nine-variable equation.
A later [unique-namespace Jones client](research/nine-current-fresh-jones-client/README.md)
and [endpoint](research/nine-current-fresh-endpoint/README.md) avoid the
alternate provider's duplicate names; their [independent endpoint review](reviews/nine-current-fresh-endpoint-independent/README.md)
checks the theorem and recorded source/provider scope. A separate [ring
client](research/nine-current-fresh-symbolic-ring/README.md) checks the
finite-sum formula for that unique-namespace expression.
A [sharing-aware quartic seed candidate](research/nine-sharing-quartic67-candidate/README.md)
compiles the current 67/94 graph into 97 quadratic residuals with 110 natural
witnesses, compared with the tree compiler's externally counted 16,893,385.
Its [independent review](reviews/nine-sharing-quartic67-independent/README.md)
checks every gate identity, degree bound and guard externally. The all-model
zero bridge, Lean normalization and degree proofs, and new Jones/iPRA endpoint
remain open; the count does not describe the final nine-variable graph.
A further [affine-inlined seed candidate](research/nine-sharing-quartic65-59-candidate/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-quartic65-59-independent/README.md),
works directly from the current 65-gate graph. It has 59 natural witnesses
and 46 quadratic residuals; the first residual is nonzero at zero witnesses,
so its normalization needs no separate guard. These are external finite
checks. Its Lean degree and normalization proofs, all-model zero bridge and
Jones/iPRA endpoint remain open, and no final graph size is asserted.
An [explicit Lean source draft](research/nine-sharing-seed59-lean-draft/README.md)
and [independent transcription review](reviews/nine-sharing-seed59-lean-draft-independent/README.md)
cover the 46 residual expressions, but the draft has not been compiled and
its tentative degree and normalization proofs are not yet checked.
A [delayed-materialization seed](research/nine-sharing-quartic65-54-candidate/README.md)
reduces the same 65-gate source to 54 natural witnesses and 41 quadratic
residuals, by leaving selected quadratic intermediates inlined. Its [independent
audit](reviews/nine-sharing-quartic65-54-independent/README.md) checks every
gate identity, exact fixed-byte ports, residual degree and normalization
externally. The 54-witness Lean model bridge, degree and normalization proofs,
and Jones/iPRA endpoint remain open; this is not a minimality or final graph
size claim.
The corresponding [Lean source draft](research/nine-sharing-seed54-lean-draft/README.md)
has an [independent residual transcription audit](reviews/nine-sharing-seed54-lean-draft-independent/README.md),
but its tentative theorems have not been compiled.
A [bounded cut search](research/nine-sharing-quartic65-cut-search/README.md)
subsequently produced a [52-witness seed candidate](research/nine-sharing-quartic65-52-candidate/README.md)
with 39 quadratic residuals. Its [independent audit](reviews/nine-sharing-quartic65-52-independent/README.md)
checks the exact signed-pair identities, degree bound, fixed ports and
normalization externally. This is not a minimum, Lean model bridge or new
Jones/iPRA endpoint.
Its [Lean transcription draft](research/nine-sharing-seed52-lean-draft/README.md)
has an [independent source-only audit](reviews/nine-sharing-seed52-lean-draft-independent/README.md);
the tentative theorems remain uncompiled.
A [five-norm seed candidate](research/nine-sharing-quartic65-norm38-candidate/README.md)
uses the existing positive-domain norm characterization in place of the
65-gate product finalizer. An [independent finite audit](reviews/nine-sharing-quartic65-norm38-independent/README.md)
checks 38 natural witnesses, 29 quadratic residuals, the fixed bytes and the
scaled-strong-norm identity. This count is conditional on a Lean bridge using
the norm theorem's ordered-model hypotheses and natural representatives for
all four ports; that bridge, degree and normalization proofs, and Jones/iPRA
endpoint have not yet been established.
The 38-witness [Lean seed draft](research/nine-sharing-seed38-lean-draft/README.md)
and [natural witness constructor](research/nine-sharing-forward38-draft/README.md)
have separate source-only [residual](reviews/nine-sharing-seed38-lean-draft-independent/README.md)
and [constructor](reviews/nine-sharing-forward38-draft-independent/README.md)
audits; neither draft has been compiled or supplies the model bridge.
An [uncompiled 38-witness model-bridge draft](research/nine-sharing-norm38-model-bridge-draft/README.md)
and [independent symbolic alignment review](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-model-bridge-draft-independent/README.md)
match its static assignment, all 60 graph gates and 29 residuals externally;
the Lean trace, source evaluation, port casts and theorem transfer remain open.
An [uncompiled slot-extraction draft](research/nine-sharing-norm38-slot-extraction-draft/README.md),
[independently inspected](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-slot-extraction-independent/README.md),
maps the 29 vanishing seed residuals to the static gate and five norm
conditions. Its [guarded Lean attempt](research/nine-sharing-norm38-slot-extraction-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-slot-extraction-terminal-independent/README.md),
timed out after reporting unresolved `Fin.cons` goals; no extraction theorem
was produced. The subsequent graph bridge also remains open.
A [single-slot diagnostic](research/nine-sharing-norm38-slot00-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-slot00-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checked the first residual identity after explicit finite-index rewrites;
the other 28 identities and their assembly remain unproved.
A [batched check](research/nine-sharing-norm38-slots-batched-draft/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-slots-batched-terminal-independent/README.md),
subsequently compiled slots 0–5 before group 02 failed on unsimplified model
numeral casts. The later slots and final assembly were not checked.
A [numeral-cast retry](research/nine-sharing-norm38-slots-batched-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-slots-batched-retry01-terminal-independent/README.md),
compiled all 29 individual conditional identities. Its final assembly timed
out without output, so no `seed_zero_static` theorem is yet established.
An [isolated indexed-residual client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-each-slot-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-each-slot-terminal-independent/README.md),
subsequently checked that a model seed zero makes every one of the 29 literal
residual evaluations zero. The gate/norm assembly remains separate.
An [uncompiled five-norm assembly draft](research/nine-sharing-norm38-five-norms-draft/README.md),
[independently inspected](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-five-norms-independent/README.md),
isolates the five terminal conditions, assuming the pending indexed residual
zero theorem; no delta cancellation is needed for this part.
A [producer-guarded client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-five-norms-check-v2/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-five-norms-v2-terminal-independent/README.md),
now checks that a seed zero implies all five static norm targets under the
IΣ₁-model and four port-cast hypotheses. Static gate recovery and the source
graph bridge remain separate.
A [gate-batch build](research/nine-sharing-norm38-gates-batched-draft/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-gates-batched-terminal-independent/README.md),
compiled all 60 individual conditional gate constraints. Its final `gatesHold`
assembly failed on an omitted trivial conjunct, so the conjunction and source
graph bridge remain open.
A [corrected assembly](research/nine-sharing-norm38-gates-assembly-retry/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-gates-assembly-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checked seed-zero implies `gatesHold` under the same model and port-cast
hypotheses. The concrete source-graph and reverse-direction bridges remain open.
A [one-way reference-graph draft](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-draft/README.md),
[independently inspected](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-independent/README.md),
proposes recovering the original 65-gate zero from those static conditions;
the source topology, port casts and proof scripts remain unchecked by Lean.
A [conditional reference client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-static-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-static-terminal-independent/README.md),
hit Lean's interpreter memory limit before producing an object; its dependency
scan alone does not establish the combined import or the concrete bridge.
A [smaller reference-base retry](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-base-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-base-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checked topology of the actual 60-gate list from its 21 initial nodes.
Reference gate equations, carrier fields and the model bridge remain open.
A [batched reference-gate build](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-gates-batched-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-gates-batched-terminal-independent/README.md),
subsequently checked all 60 reference equations and their universal assembly
over arbitrary commutative rings. Initial static/reference agreement and the
resulting source-equation bridge remain open.
An [unsplit initial-agreement attempt](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-initial-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-initial-timeout-terminal-independent/README.md),
timed out without producing a Lean object or axiom report.
A [split initial-agreement build](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-initial-split-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-initial-split-terminal-independent/README.md),
subsequently recorded successful Lean scans and compiles for all eight stages
and the initial-agreement axiom report. The author's observed PASS output did
not retain a numeric outer exit; no such exit is inferred. A subsequent
[terminal-agreement client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-terminals-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-terminals-terminal-independent/README.md),
checks that the static gate constraints identify all six required terminal
values with their reference nodes in IΣ₁ models. Its author observed outer
exit 0; the inherited initial producer's numeric outer exit remains uncaptured.
A later checked composition derives the carrier zero, and a generic-port
client translates it to source-expression zero under explicit cast hypotheses.
The reverse source/seed construction remains separate.
A [separate field client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-fields01-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-fields01-terminal-independent/README.md),
checks that reference nodes 61 and 44 equal the original P and Q fields over
any commutative ring. The other fields and graph/source bridge remain open.
Separate [U/W](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-fields02-check/README.md)
and [Z/delta](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-fields03-check/README.md)
clients both hit the interpreter memory limit before producing objects; their
[failure evidence](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-fields02-03-terminal-independent/README.md)
was independently audited.
Subsequent isolated [W](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-w-check/README.md)
and [delta](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-delta-check/README.md)
clients compiled; their [independent audit](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-w-delta-terminal-independent/README.md)
checks both provider routes and axiom reports. The U and Z field identities
remain open.
A [factored U-edge client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-u-edges-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-u-edges-terminal-independent/README.md),
checks three reference DAG edge identities after a bounded recursion-depth
adjustment. These support but do not establish the U field identity.
A [factored field client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-u-factored-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-u-factored-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checked the V, S and U reference projections over any commutative ring.
A [factored Z client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-z-factored-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-z-factored-terminal-independent/README.md),
also checks the last of the six needed reference-field projections. Their
combination and the graph/source bridge remain open.
A [two-stage combination attempt](research/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-fields-combine-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-reference-fields-combine-terminal-independent/README.md),
compiled the six-field conjunction. Its following conditional Carrier65
implication failed on a local `let` rewrite and was not accepted.
A [qualified-name retry](research/nine-sharing-norm38-static-carrier-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-static-carrier-retry02-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checked that agreement of the six static terminals with their reference
nodes, together with the five static norms, implies a Carrier65 zero in an
IΣ₁ model. A later client checks the terminal-agreement hypotheses from the
static gate constraints. A [composition client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-static-carrier-compose-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-static-carrier-compose-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checks that the static gates and five norms imply a Carrier65 zero at
the same shifted witness coordinates. A [generic-port client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-static-source-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-static-source-terminal-independent/README.md),
translates this to source-expression zero under four explicit model port casts.
The source-to-seed construction remains open; the initial-agreement producer's
numeric outer exit remains uncaptured.
A [forward-bridge draft](research/nine-sharing-norm38-forward-bridge-draft/README.md),
[independently inspected](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-forward-bridge-independent/README.md),
proposes constructing seed witnesses from an original zero under explicit
port-cast and positivity hypotheses; this direction is also uncompiled.
Adapted [five-norm and source-to-seed drafts](research/nine-sharing-norm38-forward-five-norms-draft/README.md)
have passed a [source review](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-forward-five-norms-draft-independent/README.md).
A [two-stage check](research/nine-sharing-norm38-forward-source-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-forward-source-terminal-independent/README.md),
compiled the five-norm theorem for the generated witnesses, but its subsequent
source-zero-to-seed stage timed out. The final whole-run provider recheck did
not run, and no source-to-seed theorem was produced.
A [staged retry](research/nine-sharing-norm38-source-to-seed-staged/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-source-to-seed-staged-terminal-independent/README.md),
compiled the source-zero-to-carrier-zero step, but the next model unit-norm
extraction timed out without output or an object. Its final composition and
whole-run provider recheck did not run.
A [further norm split](research/nine-sharing-norm38-unit-norms-split/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-unit-norms-split-terminal-independent/README.md),
also timed out without output on the first, generic-input stage; its shifted
stage and final survey were not reached. The timeout's cause is not known.
A [fixed-current-port norm client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-fixed-port-norms/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-fixed-port-norms-terminal-independent/README.md),
subsequently checked that a Carrier65 zero gives all five unit norms for
positive ring inputs at the current symbolic ports. It uses the local
irreducibility setup of an earlier checked completion. The source-to-seed
composition has since been checked by a [fixed-current client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-source-to-seed/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-source-to-seed-terminal-independent/README.md):
given the four explicit model port casts, it constructs a 38-witness seed zero
from a source-expression zero at shifted inputs. A [four-cast specialization](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-source-cast-instantiation/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-source-cast-instantiation-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checks this construction for the actual current model ports without a
cast hypothesis. A [conditional existential composition](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-existential-iff-draft/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-existential-iff-terminal-independent/README.md),
checks both seed/source and nine-variable/source existential equivalences
under an explicit universal port-cast assumption. A [cast-free composition](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-castfree-iff-draft/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-castfree-iff-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checks both equivalences in IΣ₁ models for the actual current symbolic
ports and shifted source coordinates.
An [iPRA endpoint draft](research/nine-sharing-norm38-ipra-endpoint-draft/README.md)
has passed a [source-only review](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-ipra-endpoint-draft-independent/README.md);
it follows the existing all-model bridge and native/standard transfer pattern.
Its cast-free provider and a [guarded endpoint client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-ipra-endpoint-check/README.md)
have since compiled and passed an [independent terminal audit](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-ipra-endpoint-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md).
A [forward-constructor gate build](research/nine-sharing-norm38-forward-gates-draft/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-forward-gates-terminal-independent/README.md),
checks all 60 gate conditions and their conjunction for the generated witness
assignment over a commutative semiring mapped into a ring. It does not yet
establish the five norms or the seed-zero conclusion.
A [prefix client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-forward-prefix-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-forward-prefix-terminal-independent/README.md),
also checks that the first fourteen constructed witnesses equal the original
inputs over any commutative semiring.
A [static-conditions-to-seed attempt](research/nine-sharing-norm38-static-to-seed-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-static-to-seed-terminal-independent/README.md),
failed at the final list-index identification and produced no theorem.
A [targeted retry](research/nine-sharing-norm38-static-to-seed-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-static-to-seed-retry01-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checked that static gate and norm conditions imply a seed zero under the
IΣ₁-model and four port-cast hypotheses. Source correspondence remains open.
A [combined client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-seed-static-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-seed-static-iff-terminal-independent/README.md),
checks the same-valuation biconditional between seed zero and the conjunction
of static gates and norms under those hypotheses. It does not yet relate them
to Carrier65 or prove an iPRA endpoint.
A [seed-to-source composition](research/nine-sharing-norm38-seed-source-composition-draft/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-seed-source-composition-terminal-independent/README.md),
subsequently checks that a seed zero gives a source-expression zero at the
same shifted witness coordinates under the four generic port-cast assumptions.
A [concrete-port specialization](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-seed-source-draft/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-seed-source-terminal-independent/README.md),
then discharges those casts using the four separately checked current-port
projections, without expanding the coefficients. The converse and iPRA transfer
remain separate.
A [current-port specialization attempt](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-ports-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-ports-terminal-independent/README.md),
hit Lean's kernel memory limit in the four-cast proof. Its failed axiom reports
are not proof evidence; actual model-port casts remain open.
A [split-port check](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-ports-split-draft/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-ports-split-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checked all four individual casts, but the universal `Fin 4` assembly
again hit the kernel memory limit and the specialization did not run.
A further [abstract assembly retry](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-ports-abstract-retry/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-ports-abstract-terminal-independent/README.md),
checked a generic finite-function lemma but still hit kernel memory when
instantiated with the concrete ports; the universal cast remains open.
A [port-parametric client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-four-variable-ports-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-four-variable-ports-terminal-independent/README.md),
nevertheless checks the seed/static equivalence from four separate pointwise
cast hypotheses without expanding the concrete coefficients. It does not by
itself instantiate those hypotheses at the actual current ports.
A [direct four-cast client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-four-cast-instantiation-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-four-cast-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checked that instantiation and the same-valuation seed/static equivalence
at the actual symbolic current tuple, without constructing the memory-heavy
universal cast. The source equation bridge and iPRA endpoint remain open.
A [prepared six-stage source check](research/nine-sharing-norm38-check-sequence/README.md)
has passed a [no-Lean preflight and independent runner review](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-check-sequence-independent/README.md).
The first run failed on unavailable update-lemma names. A [corrected retry](research/nine-sharing-norm38-check-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-terminal-independent/README.md),
compiled the generic gate algebra and forward-constructor definition before
the seed module timed out; it proved no seed or model bridge theorem.
A [split build](research/nine-sharing-seed38-split-draft/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-seed38-split-terminal-independent/README.md),
subsequently checked the 38-seed syntax and its normalization for any positive
first port and arbitrary distinguished input. Its separate weighted-degree
proof failed with unsolved goals; the polynomial-degree theorem and remaining
model stages did not run.
A [targeted weighted-degree retry](research/nine-sharing-seed38-weighted-retry/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-seed38-weighted-terminal-independent/README.md),
then passed all ten stages. It checks total degree at most four, normalization
assuming a positive first port, and equivalence between seed zero and vanishing
of its 29 residuals in the indicated IΣ₁ models. It does not yet identify
those residuals with the current graph, instantiate the actual port, or prove
the Jones/iPRA endpoint; no axiom reports were captured in this build.
A [current-port Jones client](research/nine-sharing-seed38-current-jones-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-seed38-current-jones-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checked positivity, degree, normalization and the all-model equivalence
between the nine-natural-variable expression's zero and this 38-witness seed
at input one. It does not supply the seed/current-equation bridge or an iPRA
consistency endpoint.
A [Jones/static client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-jones-static-check-v2/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-jones-static-v2-terminal-independent/README.md),
checks that the nine-natural-variable expression has a zero iff some
38-coordinate valuation satisfies the current static conditions in each
indicated IΣ₁ model. A [one-way Jones/source client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-current-nine-source-draft/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-current-nine-source-terminal-independent/README.md),
then checks that a zero of this expression yields a zero of the current source
expression at the shifted first fourteen seed coordinates. The later cast-free
equivalence and iPRA endpoint are described above.
A separate [conditional composition draft](research/nine-sharing-norm38-compose-draft/README.md),
[independently inspected](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-compose-draft-independent/README.md),
assembles the prospective seed/current equivalence from explicit extraction
and construction hypotheses. Those hypotheses, the provider route and the
draft's Lean elaboration remain unverified.
A [33-witness sign-compressed variant](research/nine-sharing-quartic65-sign33-candidate/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-quartic65-sign33-independent/README.md),
has 24 quadratic residuals. Its forward construction requires five sign
implications for every relevant model solution, with nontrivial port and
natural-subtraction transports; these remain unproved. It is therefore a
conditional candidate, not a completed reduction or iPRA endpoint.
A [generated Lean syntax draft](research/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-syntax-draft/README.md),
[independently source-audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-syntax-frontier-independent/README.md),
matches the 24 external residual ASTs and their Fin34 coordinate convention.
A [syntax-only Lean check](research/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-syntax-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-syntax-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then compiled those definitions. The JSON comparison remains external, and no
source-zero bridge or iPRA endpoint for this candidate follows yet.
A [normalization and degree build](research/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-degree-normalize-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-degree-normalize-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checked total degree at most four and the required normalization under
the stated positive first-port assumption. Its compile recorded two
unused-simp warnings. This is not an exact degree or a source-zero bridge.
A [generic square-sum client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-squares-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-squares-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that a 33-seed zero is equivalent to all 24 residuals vanishing at the
same valuation, for arbitrary symbolic integer ports in the indicated IΣ₁
models. It does not identify those residuals with the concrete source gates.
A [first residual-to-cut attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-first-cut-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-first-cut-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed on a list-membership proof after the first declaration's clean axiom
report; the failed module produced no object and its second `sorryAx` report
is not proof evidence.
A [membership-term retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-first-cut-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-first-cut-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks the first `q²` cut under the stated port-0 cast and the same
seed valuation. The other 23 residual links remain separate.
A [24-row batch inventory](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch-plan/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch-plan-frontier-independent/README.md),
records the residual order, syntactically occurring ports, signed-cut
orientations and Fin34-to-Fin33 positions for a proposed bounded proof. It
is an external inventory, not a proof of minimal casts or graph equivalence.
A [first three-row residual client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch00-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch00-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the `q²`, `q³` and `X` cut identities at one seed-zero valuation under
the stated port-0 cast. The other 21 residual links remain separate.
A [second residual client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch01-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the `Y`, `E` and `kY` cut identities at the same kind of valuation
without port-cast hypotheses. Eighteen residual links remain separate.
A [third residual client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch02-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the `ell`, `Delta` and main-root cuts without port casts. Its compile
records five unused-simp warnings; 15 residual links remain separate.
A [fourth residual client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch03-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch03-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks two more cuts and the first norm, again without port casts. Its compile
records three unused-simp warnings; 12 residual links remain separate.
A [fifth residual client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch04-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch04-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the positive gap/tau cuts and next norm under the stated port-0,
port-2 and port-3 casts. Its source header incorrectly claims no casts; the
packet records the erratum. One unused-simp warning remains, and nine
residual links are still separate.
A [sixth residual client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch05-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch05-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the transport relation, `f²` and positive `cT−r` cut. Its combined
theorem assumes all four port casts; six residual links remain separate.
A [seventh residual client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch06-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch06-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the positive `V`, normalized `S` and `S²` cuts without port casts.
Its compile records three unused-simp warnings; three residual links remain.
An [eighth residual client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch07-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-batch07-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the negative-oriented `y²−V²` cut and final two norms without port
casts. Its compile records three unused-simp warnings. All 24 row consequences
are now checked separately. An [eight-batch assembly](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-assembly-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-assembly-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks that a seed zero implies all 24 grouped conditions at the same
valuation under all four port casts. A [nine-stage converse](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-converse-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-converse-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the reverse implication under the same hypotheses. Its group logs
contain 24 unused cast-variable warnings; the final stage has none. An
[iff composition](research/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-residual-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks the biconditional at the same valuation under those casts. The
source graph bridge and iPRA transfer remain separate.
A [33-to-38 coordinate lift](research/nine-sharing-sign33-lift38-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-lift38-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the input prefix, signed slots and signed-value extraction. It does not
prove the lifted assignment satisfies the 38-seed componentwise gates: those
conditions are stronger than the signed-value equalities available here.
Ring-value gate replay requires a separate proof. Its compile records two
unused-section-variable warnings.
A [conditional double-topology client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-topology-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-topology-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks agreement of a ring-value assignment and reference on completed nodes
when both satisfy the same topological gate equations. A [first gate client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates00-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates00-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks ring-value gate targets 21–23 from the lifted valuation and static
conditions. A [second gate client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates01-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 24–26 under the same grouped conditions. A [third gate client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates02-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 27–29; its compile records one unused-simp warning.
A [six-gate client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates03-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates03-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 30–35; its compile records five unused-simp warnings. A [further
six-gate client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates04-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates04-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 36–41; its compile records two unused-simp warnings. Another
[six-gate client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates05-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates05-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 42–47; its compile records 15 simp/tactic linter warnings. A
[signed-gap client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates06-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates06-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 48–53, including the gap equality at the ring-value level. Its
compile records 18 simp/tactic warnings. A [signed-tau client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates07-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates07-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 54–59, including the tau equality at the ring-value level. Its
compile records 17 simp/tactic warnings. An [inline-gate client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates08-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates08-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 60–65 and records 17 simp/tactic warnings. A [further signed
client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates09-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates09-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 66–71 and records ten simp/tactic warnings. A [fifth signed-cut
client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates10-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates10-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 72–77, including the negative fifth-pair orientation. Its compile
records 12 simp/tactic warnings. The [final gate client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates11-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates11-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks targets 78–80 and records ten simp/tactic warnings; its unchanged source
header incorrectly says six equations rather than three. All 60 individual
gate equations are now checked under their stated hypotheses. A [gate assembly](research/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates-assembly-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-ring-gates-assembly-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that `AllCuts` implies all 60 ring-value gate equations for the same
lifted valuation; its compile records two unused-simp warnings. A [five-norm
client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-five-norms-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-five-norms-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that the same `AllCuts` hypothesis implies the five ring-value norms
for that lift. Its individual stages record 17 warnings; the assembly has none.
A [replay-to-source-value client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-lift-source-value-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-lift-source-value-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks that `AllCuts` implies zero `Carrier65` value at the corresponding
shifted inputs and model ports. A [source-syntax client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-allcuts-source-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-allcuts-source-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
transfers this implication to the source expression under four explicit port
casts and the shifted coordinates. A [two-stage seed composition attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-current-source-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-current-source-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
compiled the generic seed-zero implication, but the current-port stage failed
with a Lean interpreter memory exception under `-M2800`; it produced no
current-port object. A [factored retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-current-source-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-seed-current-source-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks that current-port 33-seed zero implies source-expression zero at
the same shifted inputs. A [forward constructor client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-constructor-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-constructor-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks coordinate projections for a 33-witness vector built from the 38-seed
positive cuts and five supplied signed representatives. It does not yet prove
the required `AllCuts` conditions or seed zero. A [first forward-cut client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-group00-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-group00-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks its first three cut equations for arbitrary representatives; its compile
records five unused-simp warnings. A [second forward-cut client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-group01-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-group01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the next three positive cuts under the same conditions. The source-zero
witness construction remains incomplete. A [third forward-cut client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-group02-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-group02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the next three positive cuts for arbitrary representatives. The inherited
initial-stage numeric outer exit remains uncaptured. A [fourth positive-cut
client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-group03-positive-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-group03-positive-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the two positive cuts in group 03; its source-dependent norm is excluded.
A [late positive-cut client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-late-positive-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-late-positive-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks `f²` and the normalized `S` and `S²` cuts. The signed fields and five
source-dependent constructor norms remain separate. A [core-norm extraction](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-core-norms-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-core-norms-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks that current source zero yields the five corresponding core norms;
matching them to the constructed witness coordinates remains to be proved. A
[natural-source bridge](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-natural-core-norms-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-natural-core-norms-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
applies the checked positive-shift equivalence to obtain those five core norms
from zero of the natural source expression.
A [conditional forward-gap client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-gap-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-gap-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the constructor's gap field assuming the corresponding core
representative equation. It records four simp/tactic warnings and does not
itself construct the representative from source zero. A [current-source gap
composition](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-forward-gap-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-forward-gap-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then supplies five representatives and the constructor gap field under source
zero and an explicit universal current-port cast. Four other representative
equalities and the remaining constructor conditions still need to be applied.
A [conditional forward-tau client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-tau-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-tau-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the constructor's tau field from the generic gap and tau representative
equalities; it records two warnings and does not supply those hypotheses. A
[conditional inner-cut client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-inner-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-inner-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the constructor's inner field from gap and inner representative
equalities; it also records two warnings. A [value/negative attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-value-negative-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-value-negative-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed to elaborate the dependent `Fin.cons` valuation; neither theorem nor
an object was produced, and the final postflight did not run. A [typed retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-value-negative-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-value-negative-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks both value and negative signed fields conditional on their core
representative equalities; its compile records eight simp/tactic warnings.
A [first Q-norm mapping attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-q-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-q-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed with a Lean interpreter memory exception under `-M2800`; it produced no
theorem or object and did not reach final provider postflight. A [factored
retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-q-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-q-retry02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the constructor Q field assuming the generic core Q norm, using the
checked 38-witness reference terminal. It records one unused-simp warning. A
[W-norm mapping](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-w-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-w-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the constructor W field assuming generic core W=1; its second stage
records four simp/tactic warnings. A [P-norm mapping attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-p-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-p-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed with a Lean interpreter memory exception in its first stage; it produced
no object and did not run its second stage or final postflight. The remaining
norm mappings are separate. A [factored P attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-p-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-p-retry01-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed on a tau cast-shift mismatch, with no object or postflight; its `sorryAx`
report is not proof evidence. A [targeted P retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-p-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-p-retry02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the constructor P field assuming core P=1 and the tau representative.
A [strong-norm mapping attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-strong-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-strong-partial-frontier-independent/README.md),
compiled its conditional 38-terminal stage, but its second stage failed on
recursion depth and produced no object; its `sorryAx` report is not proof
evidence and final postflight did not run. An [explicit-coordinate retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-strong-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-strong-retry01-partial-frontier-independent/README.md),
again compiled stage one but its second stage timed out after 120.224 seconds
without an object or final postflight; the cause of the timeout is unknown.
A [split-coordinate attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-strong-split-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-strong-split-partial-frontier-independent/README.md),
compiled stage one but hit Lean's 200000-heartbeat limit at coordinate 22;
later stages and final postflight did not run, and its `sorryAx` report is not
proof evidence. An [abstract projection client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-abstract-projection22-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-abstract-projection22-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks coordinate 22 while keeping the 38-coordinate tuple abstract. It
does not establish the strong norm. An [additional projection client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-abstract-projections28-32-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-abstract-projections28-32-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the corresponding abstract projections for coordinates 28 and 32;
their application to the strong norm is checked by a [factored retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-strong-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-strong-retry02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md).
It derives the constructor strong field assuming core strong=1 and records one
unused-simp warning; composition with source zero remains separate.
A [node-76 client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-node76-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-node76-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the conditional topology equality and generic reference `V²−y²`
identity. The sign reversal and constructor binding needed for the U norm
remain separate. A [negative-sign attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-negative-node76-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-negative-node76-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed because `dsimp` made no progress; it produced no object or final
postflight, and its `sorryAx` report is not proof evidence. A [tactic-only retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-negative-node76-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-negative-node76-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the sign relation assuming the explicit `y²−V²` representative; the
33-coordinate and U-norm applications remain separate. A [node-78 factor](research/nine-sharing-sign33-node78-factor-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-node78-factor-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks a generic graph identity with one tactic-sequencing warning; its U-norm
application is checked by a [conditional U client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-u-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-norm-u-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md).
It derives the constructor U field assuming core U=1 and the negative
representative equation, with one tactic-sequencing warning. All five
conditional norm mappings are now checked; their assembly with source-derived
representatives remains separate. A [conditional forward AllCuts assembly](research/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-all-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-forward-all-cuts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks all eight groups for the same constructed valuation assuming the five
core norms and five representative equalities. Supplying those hypotheses
from source zero is checked by a [current-source AllCuts client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-forward-all-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-forward-all-cuts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md):
natural source zero yields an existential AllCuts witness under an explicit
universal current-port cast. A [source-to-seed client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-source-seed-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-source-seed-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then derives a 33-seed zero with distinguished input 1 under the same explicit
universal cast. A [four-cast specialization](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-source-seed-casts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-source-seed-casts-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
discharges that hypothesis for the current ports: natural source zero yields
an existential 33-seed zero with leading 1 in each indicated IΣ₁ model.
A [source/seed equivalence](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-seed-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-seed-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
composes both directions: existence of a natural source zero is equivalent to
existence of a current 33-seed zero with leading 1 in those models, without
explicit port-cast hypotheses. Jones transfer and iPRA proof existence remain
separate. An [independent 33-seed Jones client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-jones-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-jones-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the symbolic nine-variable Jones zero iff 33-seed existential zero in
those models. A [Jones/source composition](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-jones-source-iff-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-jones-source-iff-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks both directions between this nine-variable zero and source zero in the
indicated IΣ₁ models. The iPRA endpoint and numeric export remain separate.
A [generic reference-ell client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-reference-ell-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-reference-ell-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the ring-level node 30 identity used by a proposed P-norm route; it
records one tactic-sequencing warning. A [constructor-ell attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-constructor-ell-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-constructor-ell-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
timed out after 120.206 seconds with an empty log and no object or final
postflight; the cause is unknown. Its model-topology and constructor connections
remain separate. A [node-30 topology client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-static-node30-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-static-node30-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the relevant same-valuation equality conditional on `gatesHold`; its
constructor application remains separate. A [factored constructor attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-constructor-ell-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-constructor-ell-retry01-partial-frontier-independent/README.md),
checked its coordinate projections but failed its second stage on a
cast-of-successor mismatch; that stage has no object, its `sorryAx` report is
not proof evidence, and final postflight did not run. A [ring-map retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-constructor-ell-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-constructor-ell-retry02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks the constructor's `v21` against shifted core `ell`, recording one
