unused-simp warning. The P-norm mapping remains separate.
An [uncompiled sign draft](research/nine-sharing-sign33-signs-draft/README.md),
[independently inspected](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-signs-draft-independent/README.md),
uses the maintained first-index theorem directly and derives the sign
implications from explicit bounds. Their natural-to-ring transfer, port casts
and connection to the seed norms remain open.
A [standalone ordered-ring client](research/sign33-ordered-ring-standalone-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/sign33-ordered-ring-terminal-root/README.md),
checks the three conditional lemmas that imply those five signs from the
explicit intermediate inequalities and auxiliary norm. It does not derive
their hypotheses for model solutions or construct the 33 witnesses.
A [model strong-norm client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-strong-f-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-strong-f-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed on the final `1+1 ≤ f` versus `2 ≤ f` numeral normalization. It
produced no object; the resulting `sorryAx` report is not proof evidence.
A [targeted numeral retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-strong-f-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-strong-f-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checked that the strong norm and the four stated positivity/equality
hypotheses imply `2 ≤ f` in IΣ₁ models. Its compile has one linter suggestion;
the remaining sign transports and 33-witness zero bridge are still open.
A [scalar and first-index window attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-windows-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-windows-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed because the `absdiff` identifier was not in scope. It produced no
successful object; the reported `sorryAx` is a failed-declaration artifact.
A [namespace-only retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-windows-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-windows-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checked the scalar and first-index window conclusions under the full
explicit positivity, mask, transport and four norm hypotheses. Deriving
those hypotheses and transferring the windows to the signed cuts remain
separate. A [current-source window client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-windows-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-windows-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then derives the three natural windows from a shifted current-source zero,
using the once-shifted high port. Binding them to the concrete seed cuts
remains open.
A [gap/tau ring-transport attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-gap-tau-ring-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-gap-tau-ring-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed because `ModelArithmetic` was unavailable in the combined `open`
command; its cascading errors and `sorryAx` are not proof evidence.
A [namespace retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-gap-tau-ring-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-gap-tau-ring-retry01-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
passed name resolution but failed on two addition-order mismatches; it also
produced no theorem or successful final survey.
A [second targeted retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-gap-tau-ring-retry02/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-gap-tau-ring-retry02-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the two gap/tau signs in the difference ring under explicit natural
window and positivity hypotheses. It does not yet bind the rank expression or
recover those hypotheses from a source zero.
An [inner/V transport client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-inner-v-ring-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-inner-v-ring-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
checks the other two signs from the explicit positive-input, half-window and
strong-norm hypotheses. Source-zero premise recovery and the final auxiliary
sign/33-witness bridge remain separate.
A [P72/sign33 provider survey](research/sign33-p72-provider-mismatch/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/sign33-p72-provider-mismatch-frontier-independent/README.md),
records one differing `ModelDifferenceRing` object among 1,007 overlapping
paths. The initially reported half-index mismatch was a mistaken comparison
to a different module; the correct half-index objects match. The differing
bytes are not a proof of semantic incompatibility.
A [fresh local absolute-difference square identity](research/nine-sharing-sign33-absdiff-square-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-absdiff-square-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
has since been checked against the current model provider without injecting
the historical P72 object. It does not by itself establish the auxiliary sign.
A [conditional auxiliary-sign attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-aux-ring-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-aux-ring-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
elaborated the mapped norm and square rewrite but failed because the
`linear_combination` tactic was unavailable. It produced no sign theorem.
A [no-new-import retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-aux-ring-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-aux-ring-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checked the nonpositive auxiliary squared-difference sign from explicit
positive `S,y` and the natural absolute-difference norm. All five sign
consequences now have conditional ring-transport lemmas; source-zero premise
recovery and the 33-witness zero bridge remain open.
A [five-witness assembly](research/nine-sharing-sign33-five-witnesses-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-five-witnesses-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then constructs five model-natural representatives under the explicit window,
positivity and norm hypotheses. Their identification with the concrete 33-seed
cuts remain open. A [current-source witness client](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-five-witnesses-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-five-witnesses-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
subsequently derives those five representatives from a shifted current-source
zero. The remaining 33-seed witness and gate assembly is separate.
A [carrier-cut binding attempt](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-core-cuts-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-core-cuts-failure-frontier-independent/README.md),
failed on three local map-add and expression-matching goals; it produced no
binding theorem. The external seed indices and graph remain separate.
A [normalization retry](research/nine-sharing-sign33-current-core-cuts-retry01/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-sign33-current-core-cuts-retry01-terminal-frontier-independent/README.md),
then checks the five representative equalities against the current
Carrier65 core fields. The external 33-seed graph and remaining coordinate
assembly are still unbound in Lean.
An [uncompiled generic gate-model draft](research/nine-sharing-gate-model-draft/README.md),
[independently inspected](reviews/nine-sharing-gate-model-draft-independent/README.md),
separates signed-pair algebra and conditional trace replay from generated
forward witnesses for the 52- and 54-witness seeds. The actual trace,
port-image and source-graph proofs are not yet supplied.
A [generic topology client](research/nine-sharing-norm38-topology-check/README.md),
[independently audited](reviews/nine-sharing-norm38-topology-terminal-independent/README.md),
checks conditional topological replay and the static-assignment fixed point;
it does not establish the concrete graph/source correspondence.
A [checked source-to-port join](research/nine-current-fresh-port-join-retry/README.md),
[independently reviewed](reviews/nine-current-fresh-port-join-independent/README.md),
identifies the current natural expression used by the endpoint with the
positive-shifted expression at all four symbolic decoded ports. This does not
formalize gzip or PDF contents. An [independent source-level count linkage](reviews/nine-current-fresh-seed-count-link-independent/README.md)
also checks that the namespace-safe renaming preserves the previously counted
tree shapes; the numerical count remains external to Lean.
An additional equal-length-root replay has completed
[all 104 stages](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-equal-length-stages-102-104-monitored/README.md):
84 output objects match byte-for-byte, and 20 match after one equal-length
source-path substitution per object. Differing direct hashes are mapped only
to previously checked predecessors. Both endpoint objects and their named
axiom reports match. This is not a Lean object-format theorem or a complete
ancestry audit.
A new guarded peer has compiled its first ten stages, with evidence for
[stages 1–5](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-peer-first-five/README.md) and
[6–10](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-peer-stages-six-ten/README.md). Several
objects differ in bytes: the stage-two difference is localized to a source
pathname, while a stage-six size/layout discrepancy remains unexplained by
any proved Lean serialization semantics. These prefixes do not establish a
matched endpoint replay.
The same peer has since compiled and compared
[stages 11–15](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-peer-stages-eleven-fifteen/README.md),
[16–20](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-peer-stages-sixteen-twenty/README.md), and
[21–25](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-peer-stages-twentyone-twentyfive/README.md).
These remain scoped prefix observations with some differing object bytes.
The [26–30 peer stages](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-peer-stages-26-30/README.md)
match their author output objects, source hashes and recorded direct guards;
the complete endpoints and inherited ancestry remain unchecked by that packet.
The subsequent scoped peer comparisons through
[stages 61–65](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-peer-stages-61-65/README.md) also
match their output objects and recorded direct guards. In
[stages 66–70](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-peer-stages-66-70/README.md), four
outputs match and the last differs in its embedded source pathname while
importing earlier objects with differing hashes.
The original peer also compiled the CURRENT2011 endpoint and its
[axiom-report client](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-peer-stage-81/README.md),
but earlier differing-hash imports prevent an exact full-chain match claim.
A separate [source-evidence inspection](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-endpoint-source-evidence/README.md)
checks the endpoint statements and the source-level expression binding; it
does not reproduce the reported builds or interpret the document's bytes.
A [build preflight](research/nine-variable-ipra/bounded-build-preflight/README.md)
identifies 103 missing project modules across the two source-declared closures
on the selected historical provider. Existing object availability and the
sampled source comparisons do not authenticate its full dependency closure.
The first five author-side stages of the resumable build passed independent
artifact checks: [stages 1–2](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-first-two-stages-author-audit/README.md)
and [stages 3–5](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-3-5-author-audit/README.md).
The second uses a source-bound warning baseline and has an earlier rejected
object with a different unexplained hash. These are intermediate results, not
a replay of either endpoint. The later [stages 6–9](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-6-9-author-observation/README.md)
also passed on the same author overlay; their source, log, receipt and warning
baseline hashes were retained in an author-side observation. The same is true
for [stages 10–14](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-10-14-author-observation/README.md);
and for [stages 15–17](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-15-17-author-observation/README.md)
and [18–20](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-18-20-author-observation/README.md).
The author observations for [stages 21–23](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-21-23-author-observation/README.md)
cover model packing, master and nine-variable reduction. Observations for
[stages 24–30](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-24-30-author-observation/README.md),
[31–35](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-31-35-author-observation/README.md)
and [36–40](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-36-40-author-observation/README.md)
cover subsequent syntax and representation modules. The author observations
for [stages 41–50](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-41-50-author-observation/README.md)
include the fixed syntax and quartic model bridge; [stages 51–60](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-51-60-author-observation/README.md)
cover equality-only theory-transfer modules. The author observations for
[stages 61–69](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-61-69-author-observation/README.md)
include the endpoint reduction under two model obligations, while
[stages 70–74](reviews/nine-variable-ipra-stages-70-74-author-observation/README.md)
include the internal Jones reduction.
These observations do not establish a paired replay or full closure.

The newer [13-variable degree-36 equation](equation36.pdf) has an
87-operation evaluator. Its exact fixed-port literal now has a no-premise
[native endpoint](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRASquareSumsNative.lean): the
candidate native theory proves its no-zero sentence equivalent to the
negation of the mapped Foundation ZFC refutation predicate. Both focused
six-module suffix replays and their independently run guarded comparisons
match; see the [comparison](reviews/ipra-square-sums-native-verification/README.md).
The theorem uses an all-model same-valuation zero equivalence to the previously
checked 86-operation construction. The comparison checks the six rebuilt
sources, full selected overlay, direct inputs and companions, but does not
establish a full current-source closure. Its original 258/848 project ancestry,
package-transitive ancestry and dynamic imports remain inherited.

The [14-variable degree-30 equation](equation30.pdf) has a 91-operation
evaluator. Its exact fixed-port literal also has a no-premise
[native endpoint](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRASquareWitness14Native.lean)
in the candidate theory. The model argument gives an arity-changing
no-zero equivalence with the degree-36 expression, and the generic bridge
transfers the endpoint. Both focused eight-module suffix replays and their
independently run guarded comparisons match; see the
[comparison](reviews/ipra-square14-native-verification/README.md). These checks
are on the historical model-provider chain, not a full current-source closure;
the original 258/848 project, package-transitive and dynamic-import ancestry
remains inherited.

The [direct-square degree-30 variant](equation30-direct.pdf) uses 14 positive

in the candidate theory. Both focused eight-module suffix replays and two
independent guarded comparisons match; see the
[comparison](reviews/ipra-square14-direct-native-verification/README.md).
The result uses the checked fixed-port model equivalence and the generic
arity-changing no-zero bridge. The checks are on the historical model-provider
chain rather than a full current-source closure; 258/848 project,
package-transitive and dynamic-import ancestry remains inherited.

The exact direct-square native endpoint also occurs in the independently
audited fresh AQ29 parent pair. A subsequent [five-module historical author extension](reviews/ipra-standard-direct14-guarded-author/README.md)
compiled its standard-iPRA client and selected translated-consistency

A later [historical paired extension](reviews/ipra-direct14-historical-pair-terminal/README.md)
matched all five additions and passed an [independent complete-result audit](reviews/ipra-direct14-historical-pair-independent-audit/README.md).
The separate [finite display binding](reviews/direct14-pdf-historical-lean-binding/README.md)
retains the manual decoder and rank-recipe boundary.

The 13-variable degree-36 and degree-56 equations also have a [joint guarded
historical author extension](reviews/ipra-standard-13-frontier-guarded-author/README.md)
with native and standard-iPRA endpoint clients. An [independent artifact audit](reviews/ipra-standard-13-frontier-guarded-author-independent-audit/README.md)
passed. A later [historical pair](reviews/ipra-frontier13-historical-pair-terminal/README.md)
matched all eight additions and passed an [independent complete-result audit](reviews/ipra-frontier13-historical-pair-independent-audit/README.md).
Their [finite display binding](reviews/frontier13-pdf-historical-lean-binding/README.md)
has a separate review and retains the decoder and rank-recipe boundary.

A distinct [13-variable, 69-operation candidate](research/thirteen69-model-bridge/PLAN.md)
has exact degree 174 and a different phase-split2471 four-port tuple. An
[independently audited model seed](reviews/thirteen-model-strong-seed-independent-audit/README.md)
supplies the required extra Pell divisibility factor, but the candidate's
all-model reverse construction, full tuple calibration and iPRA endpoint
remain open. The previously checked 13-variable endpoints do not by
themselves establish this candidate's equivalence. A distinct instance using
the 2011 tuple now has an [audited 69-row model binding](reviews/thirteen69-dag-binding-independent-audit/README.md)
and [independently checked degree 174](reviews/thirteen69-degree2011-independent-audit/README.md).
One model direction has a [paired, independently reviewed fixed-port theorem](reviews/thirteen-model-exact-port-independent-audit/README.md).
Newly merged source declares both model directions and iPRA endpoints, but
their subsequent 338-module coherent successor pair and 69-node semantic
binding have now passed an [independent artifact audit](reviews/thirteen-model-endpoint-successor-pair-independent-audit/README.md).
This verifies the endpoint declarations for their own expression and a
model-level zero equivalence to the 69-node graph, subject to the audit's
inherited provider and dynamic-loading qualifications. A separate [internal
iPRA client for the exact 69-node syntax](research/thirteen-model-dag-ipra-followup/README.md)
has since passed a paired replay and [independent artifact and statement audit](reviews/thirteen-model-dag-ipra-independent-audit/README.md).
It includes native, standard and reference theory endpoints, and the standard
endpoint for the Gödel–Gentzen translation of the selected Foundation ZFC
consistency sentence. The 69-operation count is for the positive-coordinate
DAG; its object no-zero syntax uses the positive shift. A [decimal-seed
display](equation69-2011.pdf) and its [external transcription check](reviews/equation69-2011-display/README.md)
are separate from the Lean theorem and retain the fixed-recipe interpretation
boundary. A separate [independent display review](reviews/equation69-2011-display-independent-audit/README.md)
passed, including PDF reproducibility and exact graph-row comparisons.

The later-source [lower-degree suffix](research/thirteen-model-lowdegree-suffix/README.md)
also has paired and independently audited native, standard and reference
iPRA endpoints for nine current-2011 source expressions. Their separate
JSON-graph bindings, reported evaluator sizes and exact degrees have not yet
been independently established, and translated-consistency clients remain
separate obligations for the full family. Two instances have since received
separate exact graph and iPRA audits: [75 operations, degree 96](reviews/thirteen75-model-binding-independent-audit/README.md)
and [84 operations, degree 92](reviews/thirteen84-model-binding-independent-audit/README.md),

translated-consistency comparison. The [75-operation display](equation75-2011.pdf)
passed a separate [external transcription and PDF audit](reviews/equation75-2011-display-independent-audit/README.md),
and the [84-operation display](equation84-2011.pdf) passed its own
[independent review](reviews/equation84-2011-display-independent-audit/README.md),
with the same fixed-recipe interpretation boundary as the 69-operation page.

The historical provider and inherited ancestry qualifications remain; exact

The [35-variable degree-6 equation](equation6-35.pdf) has a no-premise

is discharged at the fixed port. Historical provider and inherited ancestry

The [34-variable degree-6 equation](equation6-34.pdf) has a no-premise

The [33-variable degree-6 equation](equation6-33.pdf) has a no-premise

The [31-variable degree-6 equation](equation6-31.pdf) has a no-premise

The [43-variable quartic equation](equation4-43.pdf) and [32-variable
degree-6 equation](equation6-32.pdf) have no-premise native endpoints for

The [42-variable quartic equation](equation4-42.pdf) has a no-premise

It uses the final 31-native provider with missing objects from the conditional
quartic provider; overlapping transitive, package and dynamic ancestry remain

The [30-variable degree-eight equation](equation8-30.pdf) has a no-premise
[native endpoint](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAAQ30Native.lean) for its
exact shifted fixed-port literal. Its [focused paired replay](research/adaptive-aq30-proof/NATIVE.md)
matched eleven modules and all nine rebuilt model objects. The model theorem
proves the needed W positivity from the actual zero for arbitrary natural port
vectors. Historical provider and inherited ancestry qualifications remain;

The [41-variable quartic equation](equation4-41.pdf) has a no-premise
