The candidate [native object theory](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeTheory.lean)
uses the existing finite primitive-recursive function-code language, native
defining equations, successor and order axioms, intuitionistic Leibniz
equality and parameterized quantifier-free induction. Its guarded author and
independent suffix replays have passed. [`IPRAStandardTheory`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAStandardTheory.lean)
now identifies it with a standard presentation: `standardTheory` takes
reflexivity, replacement `x = y → A(x) → A(y)` for **atomic** `A` only, the same
defining, successor and order axioms, and successor-step `A(0) → ∀x (A(x) →
A(S x)) → ∀x A(x)` quantifier-free induction with arbitrary parameters.
`leibniz` derives replacement for every formula from the atomic instances, and
`provable_iff : standardTheory ⊢ φ ↔ nativeTheory ⊢ φ` follows with the
existing successor-step conversions. [`IPRAStandardCurrentEndpoint`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAStandardCurrentEndpoint.lean)
states the 14-variable equivalence in `standardTheory`, both with the native
negation of the mapped refutation predicate and with the Gödel–Gentzen image of
Foundation's ZFC consistency sentence. On 2026-11-01 it built with Lake's
ordinary trace-checked rebuild from the Lean sources of commit `66da84a9e7`
(92 project modules recompiled), and its
[checks](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAStandardCurrentEndpointChecks.lean)
report only `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`. This is an
author build, not an independent replay packet. A later [16-module guarded
author extension](reviews/ipra-standard-current14-author-trial/README.md)
over the fresh AQ29 author provider passed, as did a separate [artifact audit](reviews/ipra-standard-current14-author-independent-audit/README.md).
It is a historical hybrid. A later [paired replay](reviews/ipra-standard-current14-historical-pair-terminal/README.md)
matched all 16 additions and passed an [independent complete-result audit](reviews/ipra-standard-current14-historical-pair-independent-audit/README.md).
A later single [fresh-project rebuild](research/current14-standard-fresh-rebuild-preflight/README.md)
compiled the 1,000-module source-declared closure from an empty project-object
tree, with an observed launcher exit of zero and no source drift at its final
check. An independent [artifact audit](reviews/current14-fresh-base-independent/README.md)
passed for its recorded sources, direct imports, outputs and target axiom
reports. This is one rebuild, not a paired replay; package-transitive,
dynamic and transient-mutation ancestry remains inherited. A subsequent
[matrix-free port assembly and endpoint](research/current14-mf-base-only-endpoint-attempt02/README.md)
compiled together on that base. Its Lean theorem gives an outer-level
existential port tuple satisfying the four structured printed recipes and an
`iPRA ⊢ no-zero ↔ ¬refutation` derivability statement for that same tuple.
The runner exited 0 and a separate [artifact audit](reviews/current14-mf-base-only-attempt02-terminal/README.md)
passed for both clients and their six axiom reports. This is one client run,
followed by a [same-source replay](reviews/current14-mf-base-only-replay-terminal-independent/README.md)
that exited successfully and matched both objects, direct dependencies and
axiom reports. Both runs use the same base and package provider; the mapped
Foundation refutation predicate and inherited external ancestry retain their
stated interpretation and qualifications. A
separate [translated-consistency client](research/current14-mf-translated-endpoint/README.md)
has also compiled on the same tuple and base, giving iPRA derivability of the
biconditional with Foundation's mapped Gödel–Gentzen-translated consistency
sentence; its actual process exit and terminal artifact audit passed.
The first supervised [printed-port extension attempt](reviews/current14-extension-v2-failure/README.md)
exited 1 before compiling a module because its dependency guard rejected two
identical `Init` paths reported by Lean. A reviewed v3 retry compiled all 61
extension modules, exited successfully, and passed a separate terminal
artifact audit; it is not a paired replay and inherits package-transitive and
dynamic ancestry. The subsequent coherent two-client build compiled the
four-port assembly but failed when importing the structured endpoint because
Lean reported a duplicate `Matrix.map.eq_1` declaration. The subsequent
matrix-free join above avoids that conflicting dependency closure.
A [rational-leaf 64-operation client](research/rational-carrier64-lean-identity/README.md)
checks that its complete literal graph equals the 65-row Carrier65 arithmetic
front over any commutative ring under `beta*Urank=MF` and `Wrank=MC−MF`. Its
[independent audit](reviews/rational-carrier64-lean-identity-independent/README.md)
checks the row transcription and direct artifacts without replaying Lean.
A checked [rational specialization](research/rational-carrier64-lean-specialization/README.md)
now gives pointwise and positive-integer-zero equivalence for arbitrary integer
ports with nonzero beta. A separate [current-base identity](research/rational-carrier64-current14-base-draft/README.md)
binds the 65-row value to integer evaluation of the fresh current14 expression
without the historical Carrier65 object. A [current-base rational
specialization](research/rational-carrier64-current14-specialization/README.md)
checks the same-valuation positive-integer-zero equivalence for arbitrary
integer ports with nonzero beta. A separate [current14 beta theorem](research/rational-carrier64-current14-actual-port-split/README.md)
checks nonzeroness of the actual first port without expanding it. A first
[combined actual-port attempt](research/rational-carrier64-current14-actual-port/README.md)
hit Lean's memory limit before producing an object. A split [actual-port
external client](research/rational-carrier64-current14-actual-external/README.md)
now checks the same-positive-integer-valuation zero equivalence at the exact
current14 ports. A [same-base pair](research/rational-carrier64-current14-pair/README.md)
checks that external equivalence together with the existing iPRA derivability
for the integer `noZero14` sentence. It is not an internal rational-leaf
sentence. A [cleared integer client](research/rational-carrier64-cleared-ipra/README.md)
selects the existing normalized 65-row syntax, proves its no-zero sentence
definitionally equals `noZero14`, and reuses its iPRA derivation. Rational-to-
integer evaluation remains external. A [cleared-pair client](research/rational-carrier64-cleared-pair/README.md)
checks that external same-valuation equivalence together with the iPRA
derivation for the explicit cleared integer sentence on the same base. Its
[same-source replay](research/rational-carrier64-cleared-pair-replay/README.md)
matched the original object and log using the same captured providers.
Displayed-file binding and an internal rational-language transfer remain
separate, and the maintained integer-leaf count is unchanged. A [finite affine screen](research/rational-carrier64-affine-leaf-screen/README.md)
found no sub-64 replacement within its stated target-row family and sampled
moduli at the separate reversed2521 ports; it does not cover current14 fixed
ports or prove a general operation lower bound.
The separate finite PDF binding retains its decoder and rank-recipe boundary.
A [structural source checker](research/current14-tex-syntax-structural-audit/README.md),
with a separately implemented [independent review](reviews/current14-tex-syntax-structural-independent/README.md),
compares the displayed positive domain, 22 arithmetic abbreviations and boxed
equation with `expressionAt`, including coordinate and port positions. It
strengthens the earlier manual comparison but does not interpret PDF, verify
the printed coefficient recipes or supply a Lean file-semantics theorem.
A [checked decimal-seed theorem](research/current14-seed-lines-binding/README.md)
now identifies 715 explicit Lean decimal digits with the imported machine seed;
its [independent review](reviews/current14-seed-lines-binding-independent/README.md)
separately checks those digits against the TeX and PDF text extraction. This
does not formalize PDF semantics or authenticate the inherited seed object's
source ancestry.
An existing [four-port recipe assembly](research/current14-literal-port-assembly/README.md)
states structured Lean identities for all four printed coefficient recipes
using captured providers. A separately developed [integer low-mask binding](research/current14-lowmask-integer-binding/README.md)
reproves the low-mask slot using a generic radix support identity. The newer
matrix-free join checks the mathematical recipe transcription against the
endpoint tuple; its correspondence to TeX and compressed coefficient files,
and any formal PDF semantics, remain separate. The results retain the provider
qualifications recorded in their receipts.
The language keeps `<` as a
symbol fixed by its explicit definition `x < y ↔ ltBit(x,y) = 1`. In the
equality-only language, [`IPRAReferenceCurrentEndpoint`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAReferenceCurrentEndpoint.lean)
states the same equivalence in the reference theory with `<` erased; it and
the `nativeToReference` closure also have an ordinary Lake author build on
2026-11-01, without independently authenticated full dependency ancestry (see
[IPRA_AXIOMATIZATION.md](IPRA_AXIOMATIZATION.md)). The candidate is neither classical
`PRATheory.PRA` nor merely the negative translation of that theory. The
historical package provider is now partly unavailable. A rebuilt Foundation
object already differs from its recorded historical hash; a successful replay
mixing the new objects with historical project objects would not by itself
establish sound compatibility. The reproducible [mixed-provider counterexample](reviews/lean-mixed-provider-counterexample/README.md)
shows that even a clean axiom report and direct-import guards can miss an
incompatible transitive object. Recovery requires authenticated matching
historical inputs or a coherent rebuild of the affected dependency closure,
with package-transitive and dynamic ancestry separately accounted for. This
experiment does not establish an inconsistency in the actual project.

A fresh Foundation build's receipt records a successful inner Lake exit and
140 targets; independent checks rehashed the declared inputs and artifacts.
The outer build-driver exit was not captured. See the [provider evidence](reviews/foundation-provider-recovery/README.md)
and [independent audit](reviews/foundation-provider-independent-audit/README.md).
A two-run source-only rebuild of the AQ29 endpoint's 1,210-module declared
project closure completed from empty project object trees. After launch, upstream edits changed
`PRAWitnessCalculus.lean` and several `ZFCinPA` sources in that frozen closure,
so the result concerns the historical snapshot, with 15 recorded workspace
source drifts. The [terminal observation](reviews/fresh-project-pair-terminal/README.md)
records both successful runs and the 1,210-row match; a separate
[artifact audit](reviews/fresh-project-pair-independent-audit/README.md) passed.
Package-transitive and dynamic ancestry remain outside this build's scope.

The exact schemes are summarized in [IPRA_AXIOMATIZATION.md](IPRA_AXIOMATIZATION.md).
The induction scheme advances by the syntactic term `n + 1`, whereas the
language also has a distinct successor code. The project proves their equality
and [same-language derivability equivalence](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRASuccessorTheoryEquivalence.lean)
with the successor-step formulation, and `IPRAStandardTheory.provable_iff`
extends this to atomic-only replacement.
Classical formulas use negation-normal syntax, so interpreting a classical
implication by first expanding it as `¬A ∨ B` would give a different, generally
stronger intuitionistic formula than `A → B`. In particular, the intended
induction axioms must be constructed with the intuitionistic implication
connective rather than obtained by an unchecked syntactic embedding of the
classical induction formula.

An existing reusable starting point is
[`FoundationTheoryGG.theory_intuitionistic`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/FoundationTheoryGG.lean),
which transforms a classical proof over any suitable language into an
intuitionistic proof of its Gödel–Gentzen translation over translated axioms.
The [expanded-language conditional bridge](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IntuitionisticPRABridge.lean)
and its [literal 14-variable client](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IntuitionisticPRACurrent.lean)
now have guarded author and independent replays over a verified prefix of the
equation build. They keep the classical derivation, all translated-axiom proofs and,
where applicable, the final target-comparison proof as explicit hypotheses.
They do not discharge those hypotheses or define native iPRA; the full
equation provider and external ancestry retain the limits in their
[replay packet](reviews/ipra-theory-transfer-prefix-replay/README.md).
The separately replayed [iff translation](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAIffGG.lean)
unpacks the translated classical comparison into a comparison of translated
sides. It does not identify the translated refutation side with ordinary
intuitionistic consistency.
The classical PRA source-current construction in
[PRA_PROVABILITY_PLAN.md](PRA_PROVABILITY_PLAN.md) can supply the starting
proof. The following list records the proof plan; later entries record
progress and remaining replay obligations for its individual steps:

1. Identify the defined native candidate with a standard intuitionistic PRA
   presentation, including its full primitive-recursive language and allowed
   induction parameters. Done by `IPRAStandardTheory.provable_iff`, for the
   language with `<` as an explicitly defined symbol; the endpoint in that
   theory is `IPRAStandardCurrentEndpoint.ipra_current_iff_consistency`.
2. Revalidate the checked translated-axiom transfer after the merge and join
   it to the current equation proof. All classical PRA axiom images have been
   derived in the candidate; decidability and stability facts for the final
   consistency comparison remain separate.
3. Prove that the translated conclusion entails the intended literal
   intuitionistic equivalence, for both the no-zero formula and the selected
   arithmetized ZFC consistency predicate. It is not enough to identify these
   only in the standard model or to assume a generic conservation theorem.
4. Combine the resulting proof existence with coherent, source-authenticated
   equation and classical-PRA dependencies for each selected equation.

The focused [quantifier-free translation](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAQuantifierFree.lean)
establishes a useful syntactic special case: Gödel–Gentzen translation
preserves quantifier-freeness, commutes with finite universal closure, and
translates a classical negative equality to literal intuitionistic negated
equality. That does not require equality decidability. An author and an
independent strict suffix replay have passed; their parent and external
transitive ancestry are inherited as documented in the
[replay packet](reviews/ipra-quantifier-free-replay/README.md). This does not
address the arithmetized consistency predicate or the iPRA axiom images.
The separate [native induction syntax](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeInduction.lean)
uses intuitionistic implication and the exact `n + 1` term; it and the
[mapped no-zero identity](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAMappedNoZero.lean)
have also passed author and independent suffix replays. The native schema is
not yet a complete iPRA theory, and eligibility of translated matrices is
not a derivation of translated classical induction axioms.
The first native axiom-image result,
[successor nonzero](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeAxiomImage.lean),
has author and independent suffix replays. All five primitive-recursive
[defining-equation images](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeEquationImages.lean)
have also passed author and independent suffix replays; these do not discharge
the equality images. The separate
[basic-axiom images](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeBasicImages.lean)
now cover all three basic axioms and have passed author and independent
suffix replays. Equality reflexivity, symmetry, transitivity, arbitrary-arity
function congruence, and relation congruence have now all been translated.
The independently degree-certified 13-variable, 87-operation candidate
has [preliminary classical and native clients](reviews/pra87-translated-preliminary/README.md)
that compiled on an audited surviving-object overlay. A subsequent guarded
[38-module author and independent replays](reviews/ipra87-bundle-followup-translated-join-independent-pass/README.md)
have passed with matching rows on the classical bundle snapshot. This native
theorem concerns translated refutation; source refresh and its coherent join
with the separately established native consistency comparison remain open.

The [13-variable native translated-refutation client](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRA86TranslatedRefutation.lean)
has also compiled in a [preliminary provider union](reviews/ipra86-translated-refutation-preliminary/README.md)
with no additional theorem hypothesis. That union has a recorded partial
object audit but the original 86-side compile environment was not fully
reauthenticated. Subsequent [35-module guarded author and independent replays](reviews/ipra86-bundle-followup-translated-join-independent-pass/README.md)
have passed with matching rows on the authenticated classical bundle snapshot.
Later source refresh and a coherent join with the separately established
ordinary native consistency comparison remain open.

The exact 14-variable client in
[IPRACurrentTranslatedRefutation.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRACurrentTranslatedRefutation.lean)
now has matching guarded author and independent replays on the explicitly
historical source snapshot, and matching author/independent bundle-followup
replays have since passed. It proves native-theory equivalence of its literal
no-zero sentence with negation of the GG-translated Foundation refutation
predicate. The subsequent [literal native-consistency wrapper](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRACurrentNativeZFC.lean)
has now passed matching six-module fixed-source replays and an independently
repeated [comparison](reviews/ipra-current-native14-fixed-verification/README.md).
Those earlier runs inherited a confirmed project-object environment mismatch.
The later [73-module repair](reviews/ipra-frozen-repair-v2-verification/README.md)
rebuilds the affected project closure; both repair peers and their independent
comparisons match. The literal 14-variable wrapper was rebuilt on that repaired
parent and its [independent comparison](reviews/ipra-current-native14-repaired-verification-peer/README.md)
matched all six outputs, logs, warnings and inputs. Both 14-variable runs
recorded ten workspace-source drifts, so a source-current replay remains open.
The 848 ancestor remains author-only, with package-transitive and dynamic
ancestry inherited.

The [complete GG transfer](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAFullGGTransfer.lean)
combines every classical PRA axiom image and has matching guarded suffix
replays on source/object-matched historical parents. This proves that a
classical candidate-PRA proof translates to a proof of its GG image in the
native candidate; it does not identify ordinary intuitionistic consistency
with that translated image, and the post-merge source closure remains to be
revalidated.
The [translated induction body](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAInductionGGBody.lean)
has author and independent suffix replays: it proves intuitionistically that
the GG image of Foundation's exact `succInd` body is equivalent to the
implication-shaped induction body for the translated matrix. Closing its
free parameters has also passed author and independent suffix replays in
[IPRAInductionGGClosure.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAInductionGGClosure.lean),
including the exact Foundation `univCl'` form. Identifying that closed body
with the native finite-parameter schema and deriving the full axiom from it
remain to be checked.
The [finite-matrix construction](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAInductionFiniteMatrix.lean)
has also passed author and independent suffix replays. It constructs an open,
finite-parameter matrix from each actual Foundation open induction matrix and
proves that its translated induction instance belongs to the native schema;
it does not yet identify that instance with the exact closed Foundation body.
The later [induction-axiom transfer](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAInductionAxiomGG.lean)
completes that identification: for an intuitionistic theory containing the
native induction schema, it derives the GG image of every actual Foundation
open-induction axiom. Author and independent suffix replays passed; comparison
of their hashes and the inherited external ancestry are recorded separately.
The other PRA axiom families still require their own translations.
The [native-theory specialization](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAInductionNativeGG.lean)
has also passed author and independent coherent suffix replays: it removes the
schema-inclusion premise for the candidate `nativeTheory` itself.

The chosen refutation sentence `rho` is the actual Foundation
`provabilityPred ZFC ⊥`, and its source consistency sentence is definitionally
`¬rho`. Although `rho` has a checked Σ₁ hierarchy certificate, its displayed
definition is not simply a visibly bounded matrix. The existing classical
PRA proof of `rho → ∃ b, bnd rho b` uses classical model completeness; it is
not an intuitionistic PRA proof. Its Gödel–Gentzen translation is now
constructed in [IPRAZFCRefutationBoundGG.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAZFCRefutationBoundGG.lean):
the source-directed author and independent replays passed for the exact Foundation
ZFC refutation predicate, with matching source, object and full-log rows. This
gives the translated implication, not an unnegated intuitionistic witness.
After the witness-calculus merge, a [58-module source-directed rebuild](reviews/ipra-zfc-bound-postmerge-replay/README.md)
passed matching author and independent replays against the frozen historical
parent. Its recorded project and external ancestry is still inherited. A later
upstream refactor changed the bound endpoint and made `PRAConBForward` relevant;
a [six-module certificate refresh](reviews/ipra-zfc-bound-certificate-replay/README.md)
has now passed matching author and independent replays, with the inherited
ancestry boundary stated there. A later [historical single-provider join](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRADeepBoundJoin.lean)
has independently replayed the translated bound together with the exact ZFC
deep-stability client, discharging its bound premise. Its native equality
decision and remaining deep-matrix stability proofs were explicit there. A
subsequent [single-provider equality join](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRADeepEqualityJoin.lean)
has matching guarded author and independent replays and discharges native
equality decision as well. The sole explicit input to that comparison is the
finite-list `DeepNeed` stability-witness schema; inherited ancestry and
current-source limitations are recorded in its receipt. A [witness-family
packer](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeWitnessFamily.lean) converts an
explicit uniform stability family into the required witness, and a checked
[disjunction helper](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeDisjunctionWitness.lean)
combines one child's decision with the other's stability. Their child proofs
and common-valuation shape hypotheses remain explicit.
The analogous HA normalization uses bounded decisions and finite
double-negation shifts derived with HA induction. The iPRA construction
instead transfers classical primitive-recursive characteristic certificates
using native open decision and a recursive GG comparison, as described
below. For the exact mapped `bnd rho` matrix,
[PRARhoBoundedCharacteristic.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/PRARhoBoundedCharacteristic.lean)
derives a Σ₀ hierarchy certificate and existence of a classical PRA Boolean
characteristic code, with matching guarded replays. The subsequent native
matrix comparison is established by the bounded induction and literal client.

For open classical NNF formulas, [IPRAOpenAtomDecision.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAOpenAtomDecision.lean)
constructs native decidability and the implication from the GG translation to
the native formula when proofs deciding its actual atoms are supplied. The
matched [author](reviews/ipra-open-atom-decision-author.md) and
[independent](reviews/ipra-open-atom-decision-independent.md) replays retain a
historical provider boundary. This does not itself supply those atom decisions
or treat bounded quantifiers. A [zero-atom specialization](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAZeroAtomDecision.lean)
handles every open NNF formula built from equalities or inequalities between
arbitrary terms and zero, assuming an explicit native zero-decidability proof;
its author and independent replays match on a historical provider.
A separately replayed [subtraction implication](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRASubSuccZeroNative.lean)
proves `(Sx − y = 0) → (x − y = 0)` in the native theory without an extra
arithmetic premise. A second matched replay proves
`(u − S(v) = 0) → pred(u − v) = 0` natively; see
[IPRASubPredZeroNative.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRASubPredZeroNative.lean).
The native theory now proves **universal equality decidability** without an
extra arithmetic premise. [IPRANativeEqualityDecidable.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeEqualityDecidable.lean)
constructs the proof object; its guarded author and independent replays match.
The project, Foundation and package ancestry of the selected provider remains
historical. On that provider, [IPRANativeOpenDecidable.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeOpenDecidable.lean)
constructs free-variable decisions for all open NNF formulas and order atoms,
and open-formula stability, with no extra proof premise. Its author and
independent replays match; it does not establish bounded-search stability.
A [native selector certificate](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRABoundedSelector.lean)
now proves, for each primitive-recursive test code, that any zero witness
below a bound makes the fixed search output a zero witness below that bound.
It transfers a classical open certificate using native open-formula stability;
matched guarded replays retain the inherited provider boundary. This selector
alone does not establish bounded existential stability or characteristic
correctness. A [generic selector-stability client](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRASelectorStability.lean)
proves existential stability from explicit selector, selected-instance
stability, and characteristic-correctness proofs; its guarded replays match.
A [checked open-characteristic client](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRASelectorOpenCharacteristic.lean)
now discharges selected-instance stability using native open decision, while
retaining fresh-witness forward correctness, the selector implication and
selected-instance reverse correctness as explicit premises. Binding the
characteristic-correctness premises to the actual bounded matrix remains
to be checked. The [concrete search binding](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRASelectorSearchBinding.lean)
now supplies the selector implication from the actual `searchCode`, leaving
only the two pointwise characteristic-correctness directions explicit; its
matched guarded replays retain historical ancestry. A [bit-convention lemma](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRABitConvention.lean)
now converts `isZero(t)=0` to `t=1` for a known bit with matching replays,
without assuming that a quantified child formula is decidable. The [actual
`notCode` composition](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANotBit.lean) and a
[generic characteristic transfer](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRACharacteristicTransfer.lean)
now have matching guarded replays. The latter obtains native one/zero
correctness and child decision from the classical certificates and an explicit
per-instance child GG-to-native proof. The [bounded-child step](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRABoundedChild.lean)
now combines this transfer with the selector: for a Σ₀ child and arbitrary
term valuation it constructs stability of the guarded bounded existential,
assuming only a uniform GG-to-native comparison for that child. Its
[author and independent replays](reviews/ipra-bounded-child-replay/README.md)
match, subject to their stated inherited-provider boundary. This is an
induction step, not yet a comparison theorem for all bounded formulas.

The [structural GG-to-native comparison](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRABoundedInduction.lean)
now covers all zero-level hierarchy formulas of either polarity, uniformly
over term valuations. The open case uses native open decidability; disjunction
derives child decisions from classical characteristics and recursive
comparisons; the bounded-quantifier cases use the child comparisons and the
selector-based existential step. Matching guarded author and independent
replays are documented [here](reviews/ipra-bounded-induction-replay/README.md);
their inherited object and package ancestry remains qualified.

The [direct ZFC client](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAWholeMatrixZFC.lean)
accepts this kind of whole-matrix comparison and discharges the translated
witness bound without a separate `DeepNeed` witness list. The
[literal bounded-rho specialization](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRABoundedRhoZFC.lean)
now proves the exact mapped ZFC comparison without additional theorem
premises; matching scoped author and independent replays are recorded
[here](reviews/ipra-bounded-rho-scoped-author.md) and
[here](reviews/ipra-bounded-rho-scoped-independent.md). Those replays retain
historical project/Foundation and fallback-package ancestry boundaries.
Subsequent [package-pinned author](reviews/ipra-bounded-rho-pinned-author.md)
and [independent](reviews/ipra-bounded-rho-pinned-independent.md) replays
also match; they authenticate all 11,316 fallback files actually loaded by
the traced client and check compilations. Package and project source-object
ancestry remains inherited, and the later upstream erasure change requires a
current-source join.

The [equation-composition client](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAEquationComposition.lean)
has passed matching scoped replays, conditional on the equation and ZFC
inputs. The [86- and 87-operation endpoint declarations](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAAdaptiveNativeZFC.lean)
now compile without theorem premises in two matched 236-module fixed-snapshot
replays; their [verification receipt](reviews/ipra-adaptive-native-fixed-continuation-verification/README.md)
records full output, log and direct-input comparisons. The [ancestry audit](reviews/ipra-inherited-object-ancestry-audit/README.md)
found two inherited objects built against a different recorded dependency.
A subsequent independently matched [73-module repair](reviews/ipra-frozen-repair-v2-verification/README.md)
rebuilt the affected fixed-source project closure and rechecked both endpoint
axiom reports; its independent project-closure and source-origin audits are
linked in the repair evidence. These results remain historical snapshots, with
the 848 ancestor author-only and package-transitive/dynamic ancestry inherited.
The 1,099-source survey's 258-module source-directed rebuild has now passed
both Lean replays and two byte-matching guarded comparisons, covering 258 rows
and 10,148 guarded files; see the [comparison](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-build-verification/README.md)
and [terminal observations](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-build-terminal-observations/README.md).
Both runs saw no workspace drift initially and six edited sources at the end:
`PRAWitnessFormulaRec`, `PRAWitnessPR`, `PRAWitnessZFCAxioms`, and
`ZFCinPA.LStepAnd`, `LStepBasic`, and `LStepEx`. This is therefore a matched
fixed-snapshot result, not a source-current replay. An independently checked
[preliminary impact scan](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-postmerge-preliminary-v3/README.md)
sized a source-and-recorded-environment reverse closure at 107 modules; the
guarded successor described below has since been replayed. The 848 ancestor
remains author-only, with package-transitive and dynamic ancestry inherited.

The six-edit 107-module successor has since passed both Lean replays and two
byte-matching guarded comparisons, covering 107 rows and 11,518 guarded files;
see the [comparison](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-six-drift-verification/README.md)
and [terminal observations](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-six-drift-terminal-observations/README.md).
Both runs observed no initial workspace drift, then the later
`ZFCinPA.SeparationKernel` edit at their final scans. The comparison therefore
establishes the six-edit fixed snapshot, rather than a current-source result.
The subsequent 172-module source-directed successor has also passed two Lean
replays and byte-matching independent comparisons, covering 172 rows and
13,488 guarded files; its [comparison](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-eleven-drift-verification/README.md)
and [terminal observations](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-eleven-drift-terminal-observations/README.md)
record the results. Both started without workspace drift and ended with four
later edits: `PRAWitnessVectors`, `PRAWitnessZFCAxioms`, `ZFCinPA.UniformStatement`,
and `ZFCinPA.TranslationCore`. It therefore establishes a historical snapshot.
The 159-module successor has since passed both Lean replays and matching
author/independent comparisons, covering 159 rows and 15,183 guarded files;
see its [comparison](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-post172-verification/README.md)
and [terminal observations](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-post172-terminal-observations/README.md).
The builds began without drift and ended with later edits to `ZFCinPA.LGlueCodes`,
`LevelCodeTower`, `SetCongruence`, and `UniformStatement`, so this is a matched
historical snapshot. The subsequent 120-module successor passed matched
replays and comparisons, and its 351-module successor has now also passed both
Lean builds and two byte-matching independent comparisons. The latter covers
351 rows and 19,369 guarded files; its [comparison](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-post120-verification/README.md)
and [terminal observations](reviews/ipra-current-repaired-post120-terminal-observations/README.md)
record actual exits. Its source snapshot started with one workspace drift and
ended with seven, so it is historical rather than a current-source closure.
The inherited 848-project and external ancestry limitations still apply.

The construction proves the open-induction invariant
`x−y≠0 ∨ (y−x≠0 ∨ x=y)`. Its [base case](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRADifferenceInvariantBase.lean)
is checked, and its successor case uses the native positive
[subtraction reconstruction](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAReconstructClosure.lean)
`y−x=0 ∨ x+(y−x)=y`. The resulting
[successor bridge](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAReconstructBridge.lean)
`y−Sx=0 → (y−x≠0 → Sx=y)` and its open-term family are rebuilt on the same
provider as the quantified induction step. The [forward difference theorem](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeDifferenceForward.lean)
was similarly joined with native zero decidability. The intermediate modules
retain their stated explicit premises; the equality endpoint discharges them.

The native positive addition identities `x+0=x` and `x+S(y)=S(x+y)` have
also been checked by [direct defining-equation proofs](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAAddRecursion.lean),
with matching author and independent replays. The companion
[successor-left identity](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRASuccLeft.lean)
`S(x)+y=S(x+y)` is also checked natively with parameterized open induction.
The analogous direct native
[subtraction equations](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRASubArithmetic.lean)
`x−0=x`, `x−S(y)=pred(x−y)`, and `pred(0)=0` have also passed matching
replays; these are positive equalities, not merely their GG images.

The theory now proves zero decidability without an additional premise. The
[checked native proof](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAZeroStepNative.lean)
translates the classical PRA proof of `x + 1 = S(x)` to obtain its
intuitionistic double negation; native Leibniz equality and successor
nonzeroness then prove `x + 1 ≠ 0` without eliminating the double negation.
Native open induction yields `∀ x, x = 0 ∨ ¬(x = 0)`. The author and
independent [guarded replays](reviews/ipra-zero-decidable-native-replay/README.md)
match, retaining the historical parent-object and external-ancestry boundary
stated there. This step establishes only equality with zero; the later general
equality, order and bounded-formula comparisons are described above.
Independently, the ordinary positive
`∀x, x + 1 = S(x)` has now been proved directly from native defining equations
and Leibniz equality in [IPRAAddOneDirect.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAAddOneDirect.lean),
with matching [guarded replays](reviews/ipra-addone-direct-replay/README.md).
This proof has no object-theory decidability premise. The native theory also
proves `∀x (x = 0 ∨ x = S(pred x))` by quantifier-free induction; see
[IPRAPredReconstruct.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAPredReconstruct.lean)
and its [matched replays](reviews/ipra-pred-reconstruct-replay/README.md).
A separate [sub-self derivation](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeSubSelf.lean)
proves `∀x, x − x = 0` from an explicit zero-decidability proof by translating
the classical theorem and eliminating double negation only for equality with
zero; its author and independent replays match. The eventual composition with
the unconditional zero-decidability provider still requires an authenticated
join of their historical dependencies.
A fixed-parameter induction on one
argument cannot simply invoke its induction hypothesis at a changed parameter;
the corresponding quantified induction matrix is not quantifier-free.

It may suffice to translate the existing *classical* witness-bound proof,
instead of deriving an intuitionistic unnegated witness bound. If iPRA can
identify the translated bounded matrix with its ordinary interpretation up
to the needed double negation, and prove that an ordinary bounded witness
implies `rho`, the translated bound can potentially yield `rhoᴺ → ¬¬rho`.
Together with the other implication and the intuitionistic
`Derivation.neg_doubleNegation` lemma, this would compare the two consistency
sentences without extracting a witness from a double negation. The later
literal bounded-rho client above now carries out this plan on a scoped provider.
The pure logical final step has an independently replayed proof-producing
lemma in [IPRARefutationNegationLogic.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRARefutationNegationLogic.lean):
it assumes proofs of `ordinary → rhoᴺ` and `rhoᴺ → ¬¬ordinary` and derives
the equivalence of `(¬rho)ᴺ` with `¬ordinary`. Its
[theory-context extension](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRARefutationNegationTheory.lean)
has also passed author and independent replays, allowing both premises to be
proved in an arbitrary intuitionistic theory. The first premise, from a native
interpretation of a classical negation-normal formula to its GG translation,
is now proved generically in
[IPRANativeForwardTheory.lean](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRANativeForwardTheory.lean)
and has author and independent guarded replays. The reverse direction for the
actual refutation predicate is supplied by the later bounded-rho client.
For the literal Foundation ZFC source sentence, the separately guarded
[one-premise client](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAFoundationZFCNegationOnePremise.lean)
now proves the desired consistency comparison for any intuitionistic theory
from the explicit backward double-negation premise. Author and independent
suffix replays passed, with the inherited HA/ZF/Foundation/package boundary
recorded in their receipts; this one-premise client itself does not discharge
the arithmetic premise.
The [language-mapped client](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAFoundationZFCMappedNegation.lean)
and a generic [witness-bound logic bridge](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRARefutationWitnessBridge.lean)
have also passed author and independent suffix replays. The latter keeps all
three implications about a proposed bound as explicit premises; it neither
supplies those proofs nor yet identifies the bound with the literal ZFC one.
The [literal-bound specialization](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRAFoundationZFCWitnessBound.lean)
now chooses exactly `∃ b, bnd rho` and has passed both author and independent
suffix replays, retaining all three implication premises. It also checks the
bounded-matrix hierarchy certificate. Those premises and the larger proof
chain have not thereby been established in iPRA.
The separately replayed [structural erasure](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRABoundedErasure.lean)
proves the native bounded body implies the native unbounded body for closed
arithmetic NNF semisentences under term valuations, using intuitionistic
logic alone. The [outer-existential erasure](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/IPRABoundedExistsErasure.lean)
has also passed author and independent suffix replays in the arithmetic
language. The later mapped client and bounded-rho comparison discharge the
corresponding obligations on their scoped provider.

The repository's HA transfers and existing equation-side HA endpoints may
provide proof-system and negative-translation lemmas. HA has stronger
induction, so an HA proof alone does not establish an iPRA proof. Likewise,
Lean's classical metatheory for proving existence of a derivation is distinct
from adding classical logic to the object theory.

## Additional characteristic-code route

The [historical upstream plan](research/ipra-upstream-plan-20261031.md)
proposes reusing the classical bounded characteristic and search theorems by
negative translation, then recovering a native bounded-matrix comparison
using intuitionistic decidability. This is a possible route, not a checked
replacement for those constructive obligations: the translated bit equations,
bounded-search correctness, and quantifier cases must all be justified in
native iPRA. Its direct negation-normal reading of `succInd` must not be used
as native induction, for the implication reason explained above.

## Further questions: frontier equations without an iPRA endpoint

Recorded 3 November 2026. Every equation with a Lean Con(ZFC) proof in the
tree now also has native and standard iPRA endpoints in the tree. That covers
the README's proof-status list, the CURRENT2011 carrier forms, the two
nine-unknown equations, and AQ22–AQ40. Two groups remain outside that
statement:

- **The ordinary 12-variable, 95-operation free-J graph.** Its all-model
  equivalence is a research prototype whose import closure has 462 prototype
  modules; 81 module names have several competing variants. Joining that
  equivalence to the positive-model adapter in one coherent build is the
  active rebuild described at the top of this file.
- **The delivered package frontier of
  [con-zfc-diophantine-forms](con-zfc-diophantine-forms/README.md).**
  Examples are ten positive unknowns in 75 operations at degree 402
  (package 163), and nine positive unknowns in 420 operations (package 101).
  These have manuscript proofs and finite replays, but no Lean Con(ZFC)
  proof, external or internal. An iPRA endpoint for any of them first
  needs that Lean proof.

Update, 3 November 2026. The CURRENT2011-tuple frontier point of package 188,
the 14-positive 67/94 equation (ordinary strong norm), now has native,
standard and equality-only endpoints
([`ModelCarrier94`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/ModelCarrier94.lean)). Every
other package frontier point inspected (packages 72, 101, 113, 115, 163, 170,
184 and 193: the ten-, eleven-, twelve- and thirteen-positive low-operation
forms and the nine-positive form) is built on the compact BFS943/H2082 tuple
`beta = 2^590069 - 1`. For that tuple the native source-to-halting calibration
is not yet in the tree; it is the compact44 rebuild described at the top of
this file. Kernel stages for one of them (package 163's 75/402) are tracked in
[research/ten-positive-ipra/PLAN.md](research/ten-positive-ipra/PLAN.md).

Update, 3 November 2026 (later). The compact44 calibration and the
12-variable endpoint are now in the tree
([promotion record](reviews/compact44-source-promotion/README.md),
[`Compact44/IPRAOrdinary12`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/Compact44/IPRAOrdinary12.lean)).
That closes the first group above. The compact-tuple packages still need their
own reductions to the outer data `CompactSymbolicModelIff.p` and
`ArithmeticMachineRun215`. These can now import the tree modules instead of
prototype providers.

The 94-, 92-, 91-, 90- and 89-operation 12-variable graphs followed
([`Compact44/IPRAOrdinary12Chain`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/Compact44/IPRAOrdinary12Chain.lean),
[record](reviews/ordinary12-chain-source-promotion/README.md)). The
speculative 88- and 86-operation graphs remain conditional research.

So did package 115's 13-positive 87 / 51 free-J frontier point
([`Compact44/IPRAOrdinary13`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/Compact44/IPRAOrdinary13.lean),
[record](research/ordinary13-freej87-endpoint/README.md)). Its tail factors
give the strong and auxiliary equations directly, so it reduces to the
calibrated 95-operation literal in every `IΣ₁` model.

Package 72's 13-positive 28 / 98 point followed on 4 November 2026
([`Compact44/IPRAOrdinary72`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/Compact44/IPRAOrdinary72.lean),
[record](research/ordinary13-p72-endpoint/README.md)). It needed its own
soundness and converse, because it permits transport quotient one, which no
free-J zero has.

- Its forward direction reaches halting through the six
  `CompactLeafFacts.LeafFacts`, on which the compact44 halting chain is now
  also stated (`Compact44/Facts/`).
- So any further compact44 equation needs only those six facts and a
  converse.

Its fourteen-positive sibling, 72's 24 / 101, followed on the same day
([`Compact44/IPRAOrdinary72P14`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/Compact44/IPRAOrdinary72P14.lean),
[record](research/ordinary13-p72-endpoint/README.md#the-fourteen-positive-sibling-p14)).
It promotes the product `E = XY` to an unknown, with residual `E − XY`. So its
zeros are P13's in every `IΣ₁` model, and its bridge composes with P13's.

Package 65's 26 / 92 followed
([`Compact44/IPRAPositive65`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/Compact44/IPRAPositive65.lean),
[record](research/fourteen-p65-endpoint/README.md)). Its main carrier is P13's
divided by `β`, so P13's window, early, exact and power sections apply.
`P72Exact.exact_ordinary` now takes the ordinary strong norm, which 65 has
natively, and P13's `exact` is its corollary.

Package 64's 30 / 83 followed
([`Compact44/IPRAPositive64`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/Compact44/IPRAPositive64.lean),
[record](research/fourteen-p64-endpoint/README.md)). It needed:

- a local norm minimum for its `Σ squares − 1` finalizer (`P64Norm`);
- an early congruence for the modulus `Y²` (`P64Early`);

retained payload/display correspondence, and filesystem/gzip/JSON/PDF
semantics remain external; no global minimality is claimed.

Two additional 76-operation proposals are under investigation. The
[independent-y graph](research/p64-qcube76-independent-y-candidate/README.md)
has 14 positive variables and externally checked degree 104. Its
[mathematical witness-map argument](research/p64-qcube76-independent-y-analysis/README.md)
has an independent [source review](reviews/p64-qcube76-independent-y-math-independent/README.md).
The generated Lean graph, [generic model bounds](research/p64-qcube76-independent-y-guarded-check/README.md)
and [ring bindings](research/p64-qcube76y-binding-guarded-check/README.md)
passed bounded checks against captured providers. The guarded [model split and main-root check](research/p64-qcube76y-graph-model-guarded-check/README.md),
[actual-zero size bound](research/p64-qcube76y-size-guarded-check/README.md),
[strict-root guard](research/p64-qcube76y-strict-guarded-check/README.md), and
[old-root nonzero guard](research/p64-qcube76y-oldroot-guarded-check/README.md)
also pass. The [model witness maps](research/p64-qcube76y-witness-guarded-check/README.md)
and [positive-zero equivalence](research/p64-qcube76y-join-guarded-check/README.md)
now check for arbitrary natural ports in the stated IΣ₁ models. The guarded
[iPRA client](research/p64-qcube76y-ipra-guarded-check/README.md) checks the
native, standard and equality-only proof-existence statements for the
symbolic Lean graph and selected mapped Foundation refutation negation. The
checks do not rebuild complete provider ancestry; external payload/display
identification remains separate.
An independent [eleven-stage receipt audit](reviews/p64-qcube76y-chain-receipt-independent/README.md)
checks the recorded direct-object chain and axiom logs without rerunning Lean.
A [source-closure plan](research/p64-qcube76y-source-closure-plan/README.md)
identifies 689 additional project modules and 42 research modules for a
prospective coherent replay on top of the currently running 14-variable base.
It is a source survey, not a replay or authentication of the existing objects.
A [restricted local rewrite screen](research/p64-qcube76y-local-rewrite-search/README.md)
found no smaller candidate in its enumerated families and proves no global
lower bound.

The separate [transport-coordinate graph](research/p64-qcube76-transport-candidate/README.md)
has 14 positive variables and externally checked degree 90. Its direct
reverse substitution requires `C<q−F`. The [changed-port and conditional
fixed-port transformations](research/p64-qcube76-boundary-obstruction/README.md)
and the [generalized fixed-port analysis](research/p64-qcube76-fixed-port-boundary/README.md)
show why a universal same-tuple inequality needs additional hypotheses; they
are conditional on suitable seed tuples and construct no graph zero. The
fixed-port analysis has an independent [review](reviews/p64-qcube76-fixed-port-boundary-independent/README.md).
Neither a fixed-port existence counterexample nor an iPRA endpoint follows.

A distinct [72-operation free-main experiment](research/p64-qcube72-free-main-obstruction/README.md)
removes the scaled gamma carrier. A paper-level forward positivity argument
and an exact partial obstruction to the direct reverse divisibility are
recorded. A [generic Lean positivity lemma](research/p64-qcube72-forward-model-guarded/README.md)
checks the required root inequality under explicit hypotheses; a further
[guarded graph theorem](research/p64-qcube72-forward-graph-draft/README.md)
now derives its hypotheses at every positive 76Y zero and supplies a positive
model-natural free-main gap. The generated [72-operation graph and forward map](research/p64-qcube72-free-main-candidate/README.md)
now pass guarded Lean checks: every positive 76Y zero yields a positive zero
of the new graph at the same natural ports in the stated models. An
[independent review](reviews/p64-qcube72-free-main-independent/README.md)
checks the graph, direct receipt chain and theorem scope without rerunning
Lean. The reverse map and zero-existence equivalence remain open. A
[full symbolic 72-zero](research/p64-qcube72-full-zero-mod-q-obstruction/README.md)
at different explicit natural ports violates the required carrier congruence
even modulo `q`; an [independent review](reviews/p64-qcube72-full-zero-mod-q-independent/README.md)
checks the argument. This refutes the direct pointwise inverse at that zero,
not an alternative witness construction or compact44 equivalence. A
[fixed-coordinate rank analysis](research/p64-qcube72-fixed-coordinate-rank-obstruction/README.md)
derives an additional necessary residue for a class of normalizations and
proves `q ∣ h` for standard-integer zeros; neither result settles the
compact44 converse. A guarded Lean [carrier-scale bridge](research/p64-qcube72-model-carrier-draft/README.md)
and graph client now prove `q ∣ h` for every positive 72-graph zero at natural
ports in the stated `IΣ₁` models. The checks retain their direct-provider
ancestry qualifications and do not yield a reverse witness map. A checked
[main-root bridge](research/p64-qcube72-main-root-draft/README.md) also
constructs and classifies the oriented Pell root while retaining its carrier
equation. A checked [large-`c` client](research/p64-qcube72-actual-large-draft/README.md)
derives the signed-half-index size bound at actual zeros with `q≥33`; its
specialization and remaining norm/window composition are separate. A
checked [residual split](research/p64-qcube72-residuals-draft/README.md)
extracts the five required zero/unit equations at actual 72 zeros.
The [conditional signed-half client](research/p64-qcube72-conditional-rank-draft/README.md)
identifies the main index with a supplied positive rank when its half-window
and natural norm identities are supplied. The [strong natural norm](research/p64-qcube72-natural-norms-draft/README.md)
and signed auxiliary natural norm are now extracted at actual zeros. A checked
[composition](research/p64-qcube72-ranked-main-draft/README.md) identifies the
main Pell coordinates at the actual graph rank assuming `q≥33`, rank positivity
and `2*rank<c`; deriving that window at graph zeros remains open. A [monolithic rank-window
attempt](research/p64-qcube72-actual-rank-window-draft/README.md) stopped at its
RSS guard without a Lean diagnostic or theorem object. A later [generic split](research/p64-qcube72-actual-rank-window-split-attempt02/README.md)
checks rank positivity and `R+1<q⁴` at actual zeros assuming the two port-mask
inequalities; an [independent artifact review](reviews/p64-qcube72-generic-rank-window-independent/README.md)
confirms its stated direct-provider scope. The first compact specialization
stopped at its memory guard; a later [opaque-port client](research/p64-qcube72-compact-port-opaque-attempt02/README.md)
checks the positive rank and quartic window at the compact44 beta and mask
ports, with arbitrary natural K. A separate
[conditional rank-half lemma](research/p64-qcube72-rank-half-draft/README.md)
checks `2R<c` in the stated models from an explicit Pell equation and rank,
scale and size hypotheses. A checked [graph-level composition](research/p64-qcube72-window-ranked-main-bridge-draft/README.md)
now identifies the main Pell coordinates at the actual rank from a positive
72-zero, `q≥33`, and the two port-mask inequalities, discharging positivity and
the half-window. A [symbolic-port client](research/p64-qcube72-symbolic-port-ranked-main-draft/README.md)
replaces `q≥33` with the compact exponential identity and uses the same two
mask inequalities. A [fixed compact-port composition](research/p64-qcube72-fixed-compact-ranked-main-draft/README.md)
now checks the rank window and main Pell coordinates for the same positive
72-zero, with arbitrary natural K. A separate checked [comparison lemma](research/p64-qcube72-first-index-comparison-draft/README.md)
derives the strict first/main index bound from explicit Pell-coordinate and
scale hypotheses without the early carrier congruence. A checked [first-norm
extraction](research/p64-qcube72-first-index-extraction-draft/README.md)
separately classifies the first Pell index at actual positive 72-zeros with
arbitrary natural ports. A [fixed compact composition](research/p64-qcube72-fixed-compact-first-index-draft/README.md)
now derives `n<R` and the first Pell coordinates for an actual positive zero
with arbitrary natural K. A checked [generic second comparison](research/p64-qcube72-half-index-lower-draft/README.md)
proves `m<2n` from the Pell coordinates and a positive remainder, without the
early congruence. Its [fixed-port application](research/p64-qcube72-fixed-compact-index-window-draft/README.md)
now gives `n<R<2n` for the same first Pell index at actual positive compact-port
zeros. A checked [same-index residue client](research/p64-qcube72-same-first-index-residue-draft/README.md)
adds `k=2n+4P*s` for that same index in the stated models. A separately reviewed
[standard-integer carrier criterion](research/p64-qcube72-fixed-compact-carrier-gap/README.md)
identifies the remaining same-witness conditions as `2n=R+1` and `U∣h` under
its explicit assumptions. A checked [generic model-level cancellation](research/p64-qcube72-free-carrier-cancel-draft/README.md)
derives `e∣9`, `Y²=e*mu`, `q⁴≤mu` and `mu∣h` from the free carrier and scale
hypotheses. A [fixed-port same-zero composition](research/p64-qcube72-fixed-compact-carrier-cancel-draft/README.md)
now supplies those divisibility facts alongside the same `n` and its residue.
A checked [conditional client](research/p64-qcube72-conditional-old-carrier-draft/README.md)
derives `2n=R+1` in those models when a caller supplies an old-carrier witness;
it does not construct one. The full model-level criterion, unconditional
first-index congruence and reverse existence remain open. A reviewed
[fixed-mask partial obstruction](research/p64-qcube72-fixed-mask-gcd-obstruction/README.md)
shows the transport, quartic window and free carrier alone permit `e=1,3,9`
and may fail `Y²∣h`; its assignments fail the first norm and are not graph
zeros. A checked [generic 3-divisible-q obstruction](research/p64-qcube72-divisible-q-obstruction-draft/README.md)
shows that the main norm and free carrier imply `U∤h` when `3∣q` and `U>0`.
A checked [actual-zero client](research/p64-qcube72-actual-divisible-q-obstruction-draft/README.md)
applies this as `Y²∤h` at arbitrary natural ports and at the fixed compact
ports, conditional on `3∣q`; it does not assert such a zero exists. A checked
[conditional no-gamma client](research/p64-qcube72-divisible-q-no-old-gamma-draft/README.md)
further excludes a same-coordinate old-carrier witness under `3∣q`; it leaves
alternative witnesses and existential reverse implication open. An independent
[rigidity analysis](research/p64-qcube72-alternate-witness-rigidity/README.md)
shows in standard integers that fixing the main scale fixes the positively
oriented carrier, and inspects the old-source path forcing q to be a power of
two. This restricts same-q conversions; it does not rule out a changed-q
witness or establish a 72 zero. A separately reviewed [partial scale swap](research/p64-qcube72-scale-swap-partial-normalization/README.md)
changes q class while preserving X and Y and satisfying transport under explicit
divisibility conditions; a conditional rigidity argument shows it cannot be
completed as a 76Y witness while also retaining c and k when the source has
`3∣q`. A checked [generic rigidity lemma](research/p64-qcube72-scale-rigidity-draft/README.md)
now proves equal main index, root and carrier in the stated models under
explicit shared-coordinate identities. A [two-zero client](research/p64-qcube72-two-zero-scale-obstruction-draft/README.md)
checks that at the fixed compact ports, if the source has `3∣q` and two
positive 72 zeros share X,Y,c,k, the second cannot satisfy an old-carrier
equation. A further checked [72-to-76 fixed-scale client](research/p64-qcube72-to-76-fixed-scale-obstruction-draft/README.md)
applies this to a positive 76Y target. A checked [first-ordinate uniqueness
lemma](research/p64-qcube72-first-ordinate-unique-draft/README.md) proves that
shared Pell base and scale interval force the same first index and k in the
stated models. Its [actual-zero application](research/p64-qcube72-actual-first-ordinate-unique-draft/README.md)
now derives equal k for positive 72 zeros at the same natural ports when
X,Y,c agree. The [three-scale 76Y application](research/p64-qcube72-to-76-three-scale-obstruction-draft/README.md)
now excludes a target sharing X,Y,c with a positive fixed-port 72 zero when
`3∣q`, deriving k equality internally. It does not establish such a zero or a
general reverse obstruction. A checked [76Y client](research/p64-qcube76y-fixed-q-not-three-draft/README.md)
now proves `3∤q` for every positive 76Y zero at the fixed compact ports, by
using the specific 76Y-to-72 witness map. It neither proves a 72 zero with
`3∣q` exists nor excludes a changed-q witness. A reviewed
[preserving-Y obstruction](research/p64-qcube72-preserve-Y-normalization-obstruction/README.md)
shows in standard integers that a hypothetical 72 zero with cube-free scale
and the stated J bounds cannot be converted to a 76Y zero at the same ports
while retaining Y; no such source zero is known. An independently
reviewed [all-moduli construction](research/p64-qcube72-fixed-mask-all-moduli-controls/README.md)
produces strictly positive outputs divisible by `N⁴` for every positive `N` at
two fixed K values, one in each q-divisibility class. It does not produce an
integer zero or settle stronger modular or p-adic arguments. The accompanying
[boundary construction](research/p64-qcube72-fixed-mask-boundary-zero/README.md)
is an exact nonnegative zero with three zero coordinates; it is excluded by
the graph's strict positivity requirement. A
[compact-window partial assignment](research/p64-qcube72-compact-window-partial-obstruction/README.md)
shows the first, main, strong and transport constraints and stated port-size
bounds alone do not force the inverse congruence. For that assignment's fixed
main coordinates and rank, a [reviewed application](reviews/p64-qcube72-partial-aux-exclusion-independent/README.md)
of the signed-half-index theorem excludes an auxiliary completion; it does not
settle other witnesses or the actual compact44 ports. The separately
[reviewed first-index analysis](reviews/p64-qcube72-first-index-gap-independent/README.md)
identifies the congruence still needed even under a conditional main-index
recovery; its finite screen concerns only a small non-compact44 family. An
[independently reviewed full 72-zero](research/p64-qcube72-equal-rank-first-index-obstruction/README.md)
now has `m=R` but violates both the old carrier and first-index congruences at
explicit arbitrary ports. Its compact mask and rank-window hypotheses fail, so
it does not settle the compact-port reverse direction. A
[separate strong-index obstruction](research/p64-free-strong-index-obstruction/README.md)
likewise shows that selected q-cube and scalar-norm constraints alone do not
force the required divisibility. A subsequent [full modified-graph zero](research/p64-free-strong-index-full-zero/README.md),
independently [checked](reviews/p64-free-strong-index-full-zero-independent/README.md),
shows that the direct strong-index inverse fails even when all 75 remaining
gates vanish at certain natural ports. It does not exclude another original
zero at those ports or settle the fixed compact44-port existence equivalence.
A separate [q-squared scale test](research/p64-qcube75-q2-partial-obstruction/README.md)
has natural-port data satisfying the first norm, main and transport equations
while `q²` divides the scale and `q³` does not. A strong-norm witness is
given symbolically. A subsequent [external argument](research/p64-qcube75-q2-all-strong-obstruction/README.md)
rules out any simultaneous strong-norm and auxiliary-unit completion for
that particular main tuple, not for the modified graph generally. This is
not a full graph zero or a fixed-port equivalence result.
For a different main tuple, a [symbolic full zero](research/p64-qcube75-q2-symbolic-full-zero/README.md)
of the 75-operation q-squared graph is now given at explicit natural ports,
with `q³ ∤ Y`. An [independent review](reviews/p64-qcube75-q2-symbolic-full-zero-independent/README.md)
checks the symbolic argument and the bounded exact arithmetic. It rules out
the coordinate-preserving inverse at that zero, but does not exclude a
different original zero at the same ports or settle compact44 existence
equivalence; the argument has not been formalized in Lean.
A separate [free-X 75-operation graph](research/p64-free-x75-symbolic-full-zero/README.md)
has an externally checked forward witness map from 76Y and a symbolic full
zero at natural ports with the freed coordinate not divisible by `q`.
The [graph binding](research/p64-free-x75-graph-binding/README.md) and
[independent review](reviews/p64-free-x75-symbolic-full-zero-independent/README.md)
check the map and witness. The direct coordinate inverse fails there, but a
different witness, fixed compact44 equivalence, and an iPRA endpoint remain
unsettled.
The [free-square 75-operation graph](research/p64-free-fsquare75-symbolic-full-zero/README.md)
similarly has an external forward substitution and a symbolic full zero whose
free squared-coordinate is nonsquare modulo 31; its
[independent review](reviews/p64-free-fsquare75-symbolic-full-zero-independent/README.md)
confirms the bounded checks and argument. This obstructs the direct inverse
at that zero, not another witness or compact44 existence equivalence. An
independently reviewed [free-first-square 75-operation candidate](research/p64-free-first-square75-obstruction/README.md)
has a full symbolic zero with nonsquare first-root coordinate at arbitrary
noncompact ports; this obstructs its direct same-witness inverse, not a different
witness or fixed-port existence equivalence.

Package 73's thirteen-positive 30 / 92 followed
([`Compact44/IPRAPositive73`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/Compact44/IPRAPositive73.lean),
[record](research/thirteen-p73-endpoint/README.md)). It reduces forward to the
95-operation free-J literal (`P73Halt`). Its converse is built from the halt
data (`P73Converse`), because a literal zero supplies only an ordinary strong
index. Package 74 evaluates the same polynomial in 93 operations.

Package 62's thirteen-positive 114 / 83 followed
([`Compact44/IPRAPositive62`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/Compact44/IPRAPositive62.lean),
[record](research/thirteen-p62-endpoint/README.md)). Its signed index step uses
`OrdinaryBlankSignedIndex.signed_half_index_blank`, for the auxiliary ordinate
`c(Tf − 1) − Rf²` of packages 50, 57 and 62. It sits between
`P72Exact.index_window` and `index_close`.

Package 57's twelve-positive 258 / 89 followed
([`Compact44/IPRAPositive57`](Lean/DiophantineConsistency/Compact44/IPRAPositive57.lean),
[record](research/twelve-p57-endpoint/README.md)).

- **Soundness.** Its fused carrier is restored by four roots
  (`P57Root.four_root`). The early congruence then needs only
  `(H·k) % U = (H(R+1)) % U` (`P57Early`). Its fused inner norm leaves the sign
  of `f` free, so the index step uses either `signed_half_index_blank` or the
  new `signed_half_index_blank_plus`.
- **Converse.** It takes `y = (y_halt + 1)/(Δic²)²`, which is integral by
  `ArithmeticOddOrdinateResidue.pair` at rank `≡ 3 (mod 4)`.

Every other compact-tuple frontier point still lacks a Lean Con(ZFC) proof.
That includes:

- 163's 75 / 402, which has a guarded-provider endpoint in research;
- the rest of the fourteen-positive Pareto row (degree / operations): 55's
  and 59's 52 / 79. Endpoints exist for the
  formulas of 183's 150 / 65 and 188's 94 / 67 and 118 / 66, but only at the
  maintained CURRENT2011 tuple (`β = 2^94517 − 1`). The packages bind them to
  admissible942 and block-mask2963. 192's thirteen-positive 174 / 69 is in the
  same position;
- the thirteen-positive 170's 154 / 72 and 162 / 71;
- 170's 74 / 438;
- 184's 104 / 174;
- the ten-positive Pareto points;
- 101's nine-positive points.

**Tuple audit (4 November 2026).** After 57, every open point is at a tuple
that the tree's Compact44 calibration does not cover. The tree's `paramKernel rho`
is the original-order K payload (raw SHA-256 `c6d523bd…`). The audit compared
the packages' coefficient files with it:

- **Directly indexed BFS943 K0** (raw SHA-256 `0a86821a…`), the same 67×8807
  layout with BFS row order: 163, 170, 171, 172, 179, 184, 193 and 194. This
  covers every ten-positive Pareto point, 170's three points, 184's
  eleven-positive point, and most ℤ rows. A research-only direct-BFS
  calibration exists (`research/direct-bfs-*`, `DirectBFS80FixedIPRA`); it is
  not in the tree.
- **`wang943-mixed-121x8801`** (β of 1,064,921 bits): 55's and 59's
  fourteen-positive 52 / 79 and 132's fifteen-integer 44 / 111. 281 of the
  618 `Compact44` modules use the 67×8807 layout constants directly. This is
  not only a change of constants: its kernel is `K = D_C + B·D_R`, with its
  own packing interface and controller
  (`55-congruence-norms/native/SOURCE_CHAIN.md`). A calibration was started
  on 5 November 2026
  ([record](research/mixed121-calibration/README.md), `Lean/…/Mixed121/`).
  Thirty-two modules, plus 22 mechanical width ports in `Mixed121/W121/`
  (about 6,700 lines), now prove the following:
  - the symbolic kernel and support facts;
  - the radix-four clause decoder and the coefficient digit form;
  - one decoded reflected band;
  - `K` in every model, as two rotated bands plus the bare part;
  - the flattened family, with its carry room `3·mass + 1 < 2^120`;
  - the four ports, with the bounds the leaves need;
  - the §3 mask facts (`popcount(MC) = d − 989`, `popcount(MF) = 989`,
    `MC` even, `MF ≡ 4 (mod 8)`);
  - the native-cell decoder;
  - **the whole `LeafFacts` halting chain at the 121x8801 ports**, from
    `symbolic_aligned` to `Mixed121Halt.halts_of_facts`: every positive
    valuation with `LeafFacts Mixed121Ports.ports v` in a model of `𝗜𝚺₁`
    gives `∃ T, first (run₂₁₅ T) = 0`. Axioms: `propext`,
    `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`.

  This is the counterpart of `CompactLeafFactsHalt.halts_of_facts`.
  `LeafFacts` is stated for arbitrary ports, so the chain was a tuple-level
  port; it needed no controller.

  **52 / 79, forward direction (2026-11-06):** `Mixed121/P55Graph`, `P55Root`,
  `P55Norm` and `P55Facts` prove that a positive zero of package 55's
  degree-52 equation at these ports gives `LeafFacts`, hence
  `P55Facts.halts`. The graph is checked against the pinned JSON by
  `research/mixed121-calibration/tools/generate_p55.py --check`. What remains
  is per-form or in the completeness direction:
  - proving `LeafFacts` from a positive zero of the 44 / 111 form;
  - the witness construction for halting ⟹ solvable (packing, rank and the
    `λ = 2341` controller);
  - the iPRA endpoints.
- **admissible942** (β of 189,269 bits; `candidates/one-page-admissible942`):
  183's 150 / 65 and 192's 174 / 69. 188 binds block-mask2963. No Lean module
  mentions the admissible942 exponent. The tree's endpoints for these
  formulas are at the different CURRENT2011 tuple.
